## [M] src/app/app.routes.ts

```ts
import { Routes } from '@angular/router';
import { authGuard } from '@guards/auth.guard';
import { roleGuard } from '@guards/role.guard';
import { unsavedChangesGuard } from '@guards/unsaved-changes.guard';
import { EmailStore } from '@store/email/email.store';
import { WellDocsStore } from '@store/well-docs/well-docs.store';
import { PresentationStore } from './features/presentation/store/presentation.store';
import { ActiveWwellStore } from './features/active-wwell/store/active-wwell.store';
import { MorningReportStore } from './features/morning-report/store/morning-report.store';
import { WwellPlanningStore } from './features/wwell-planning/store/wwell-planning.store';
import { WwellIrStore } from './features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/store/wwell-ir.store';

export const routes: Routes = [
  {
    path: '',
    redirectTo: 'login',
    pathMatch: 'full',
  },
  {
    path: 'login',
    loadComponent: () =>
      import('./features/auth/login.component').then(
        (m) => m.LoginComponent,
      ),
  },
  {
    path: 'main',
    loadComponent: () =>
      import('./layout/layout.component').then(
        (m) => m.LayoutComponent,
      ),
    canActivate: [authGuard],
    canActivateChild: [authGuard],
    children: [
      {
        path: '',
        redirectTo: 'home',
        pathMatch: 'full',
      },
      {
        path: 'home',
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/home/home.component').then(
            (m) => m.HomeComponent,
          ),
      },
      // PLANNING — open to all roles for now, mirrors left-sidenav.component.ts (no RBAC gating yet)
      {
        path: 'requests',
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/wwell-planning/wwell-requests/wwell-requests.component').then(
            (m) => m.WwellRequestsComponent,
          ),
        providers: [WwellPlanningStore],
      },
      {
        path: 'wlda-approvals',
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/wwell-planning/wwell-wlda-approvals/wwell-wlda-approvals.component').then(
            (m) => m.WwellWldaApprovalsComponent,
          ),
        providers: [WwellPlanningStore],
      },
      {
        path: 'land-affairs-approvals',
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/wwell-planning/wwell-land-affairs-approvals/wwell-land-affairs-approvals.component').then(
            (m) => m.WwellLandAffairsApprovalsComponent,
          ),
        providers: [WwellPlanningStore],
      },
      {
        // New/edit no longer route anywhere — WwellIrComponent creates a draft in place and
        // authors it inline (WwellIrEditorComponent) in the same master-detail view. The
        // read-only "View" stepper is the same story: a display mode toggled in place on the
        // detail panel (see WwellIrComponent's `detailMode`), not a separate route — the master
        // list stays visible and clickable the whole time.
        path: 'integrated-requirements',
        providers: [WwellIrStore],
        canDeactivate: [unsavedChangesGuard],
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir.component').then(
            (m) => m.WwellIrComponent,
          ),
      },
      {
        path: 'presentations',
        data: { routeId: 'presentations' },
        canActivate: [roleGuard],
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/presentation/presentation.component').then(
            (m) => m.PresentationComponent,
          ),
        providers: [PresentationStore, WellDocsStore],
      },
      {
        path: 'active-wwell',
        data: { routeId: 'active-wwell' },
        canActivate: [roleGuard],
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/active-wwell/active-wwell-view/active-wwell-view.component').then(
            (m) => m.ActiveWwellViewComponent,
          ),
        providers: [ActiveWwellStore],
      },
      {
        path: 'morning-report',
        data: { routeId: 'morning-report' },
        canActivate: [roleGuard],
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/morning-report/morning-report.component').then(
            (m) => m.MorningReportComponent,
          ),
        providers: [EmailStore, MorningReportStore],
      },
      {
        path: 'water-wells-overview',
        data: { routeId: 'presentations' },
        canActivate: [roleGuard],
        loadComponent: () =>
          import('./features/water-wells-overview/water-wells-overview.component').then(
            (m) => m.WaterWellsOverviewComponent,
          ),
        providers: [MorningReportStore],
      },
    ],
  },
  { path: '**', redirectTo: 'login' },
];
```

## [A] src/app/core/guards/unsaved-changes.guard.ts

```ts
import { CanDeactivateFn } from '@angular/router';
import { Observable } from 'rxjs';

/** Implemented by any routed component that can carry unsaved local edits (form sections open for
 *  editing, an unsaved "New IR" draft, …) — see WwellIrComponent.canDeactivate() for the reference
 *  implementation: it prompts Save / Discard / Stay and only resolves `true` once the user's
 *  choice (and any save it triggers) has actually settled. */
export interface ComponentCanDeactivate {
  canDeactivate(): Observable<boolean> | boolean;
}

/** Route-level guard for components with in-page unsaved edits — e.g. the wwell-ir master-detail
 *  page, where "navigate away" also has to cover an Angular route change (clicking a different
 *  sidenav item), not just switching the selected row in place (that path is guarded directly by
 *  the component itself; see WwellIrComponent.selectIr/viewIr/createNewIr). */
export const unsavedChangesGuard: CanDeactivateFn<ComponentCanDeactivate> = component => component.canDeactivate();
```

```ts
import { ApprovalChainStep } from './wwell-planning.model';

export type IntegratedRequirementStatus = 'draft' | 'in-progress' | 'submit-for-review' | 'in-review' | 'approved';
export type IntegratedRequirementTestType = 'flow-test' | 'pump-test';

export interface IntegratedRequirementRequestReference {
  readonly linkedRequestId: string | null;
  /** Correspondence/letter reference tied to this request — optional since older records predate the field. */
  readonly letterNumber?: string | null;
  readonly client: string;
  readonly supportingBusiness: string;
  readonly wellName: string;
  readonly targetAquifer: string;
  readonly targetDepthFt: number;
  readonly surfaceElevationFtAsl: number | null;
  readonly coordinates: string;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementFormationTop {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly depthFt: number;
  /** Optional since older records predate these fields. */
  readonly subSeaFtAsl?: number | null;
  readonly remarks?: string | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementCasingRow {
  readonly type: string;
  readonly sizeIn: string;
  readonly depthFtBgl: number;
  /** Top-of-liner depth — only meaningful when `type === 'Liner'`. */
  readonly tolFtBgl: number | null;
  readonly remarks: string | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementDevelopmentTesting {
  readonly logging: string;
  readonly mudLogging: string;
  readonly sampling: string;
  readonly flowTest: string;
  /** General Development/Testing remarks (exad_gwd_ir_header.dt_rmks) — a catch-all note for the
   *  whole Development/Testing group, distinct from logging/mudLogging/sampling/flowTest's own
   *  per-topic remarks. */
  readonly remarks: string;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementProgramDetails {
  readonly completion: string;
  /** Bit size below the last casing shoe (e.g. `8 1/2"`) — only meaningful when completion is 'Open Hole'. */
  readonly openHoleSizeIn: string | null;
  readonly acidization: string;
  readonly transmission: string;
  readonly developmentTesting: IntegratedRequirementDevelopmentTesting;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementContacts {
  readonly geologistPrimary: string;
  readonly geologistSecondary: string | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell {
  /** exad_gwd_ir_water.ep_a_num — the offset well's numeric id, and (with ir_seq) the natural key
   *  the save proc matches rows on. That table has no well-name column: production identifies the
   *  well numerically and resolves its display name elsewhere. See docs/ir/p2-ir.md. */
  readonly epANum: number;
  /** Everything below is historical test-result data — usually enriched after creation, not captured at intake. */
  readonly distanceKm?: number | null;
  readonly location?: string | null;
  readonly aquifer?: string | null;
  readonly totalDepthFt?: number | null;
  readonly testDurationMin?: number | null;
  readonly rateGpm?: number | null;
  readonly rpm?: number | null;
  readonly swlFt?: number | null;
  readonly pwlFt?: number | null;
  readonly drawdownFt?: number | null;
  readonly specificCapacity?: number | null;
  readonly tdsPpm?: number | null;
  readonly h2sPpm?: number | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementHydrogeology {
  readonly aquifer: string;
  readonly estimatedTdsPpm: number | null;
  readonly h2sPpm: number | null;
  readonly estimatedPumpDepthFt: number | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirementTestProgram {
  readonly testType: IntegratedRequirementTestType;
  readonly estimatedRateGpm: number | null;
  readonly estimatedWaterLevelFt: number | null;
  /** Shut-in wellhead pressure — only meaningful for a flow test. */
  readonly siwhpPsi?: number | null;
}

export interface IntegratedRequirement {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly code: string;
  /** Backend-assigned exad_gwd_ir_header.ir_seq — absent until the first successful save. */
  readonly irSeq?: number | null;
  readonly updatedDate: string;
  readonly status: IntegratedRequirementStatus;
  readonly requestReference: IntegratedRequirementRequestReference;
  readonly formationTops: readonly IntegratedRequirementFormationTop[];
  readonly casing: readonly IntegratedRequirementCasingRow[];
  readonly programDetails: IntegratedRequirementProgramDetails;
  readonly contacts: IntegratedRequirementContacts;
  readonly offsetWells: readonly IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell[];
  readonly offsetWellsRemarks: string | null;
  readonly hydrogeology: IntegratedRequirementHydrogeology;
  readonly testProgram: IntegratedRequirementTestProgram;
  readonly attachmentNames: readonly string[];
  readonly chain: readonly ApprovalChainStep[];
}

export interface CreateIntegratedRequirementPayload
  extends Pick<
    IntegratedRequirement,
    | 'requestReference'
    | 'formationTops'
    | 'casing'
    | 'programDetails'
    | 'contacts'
    | 'offsetWells'
    | 'offsetWellsRemarks'
    | 'hydrogeology'
    | 'testProgram'
  > {
  readonly status: Extract<IntegratedRequirementStatus, 'draft' | 'in-progress'>;
}
```

## [A] src/app/core/models/wwell-planning/wwell-planning-loading.constants.ts

```ts
/**
 * Minimum time each wwell-planning CRUD loading indicator (button spinner, section shimmer) stays
 * visible, even when the backend answers faster than that — a sub-100ms local/CDN response would
 * otherwise flash a spinner on and off too quickly to read as real feedback. Centralized here so
 * every "does this action feel like it did something" tweak happens in one place instead of as a
 * magic number scattered across stores/components.
 *
 * `WWELL_LOAD_DELAY_MS` gates a UI-only selection transition (see `createPendingSelection` in
 * `features/wwell-planning/shared/wwell-pending-selection.util.ts`) — it does not touch the actual
 * fetch. The other three are applied directly to the mutating request's own observable (via
 * `delay()`) in each feature's store, so the loading flag that also disables its button stays true
 * for at least this long.
 */
export const WWELL_LOAD_DELAY_MS = 260;
export const WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS = 400;
export const WWELL_UPDATE_DELAY_MS = 400;
export const WWELL_DELETE_DELAY_MS = 400;
```

```html
@if (mode() === 'review') {
  <div class="dyf dyf--review" [class.dyf--span-2]="config().colSpan === 2">
    <dt class="dyf__label">{{ config().label }}</dt>
    <dd class="dyf__value" [class.dyf__value--empty]="isReviewValueEmpty()">{{ reviewValue() }}</dd>
  </div>
} @else {
  <div class="dyf" [class.dyf--span-2]="config().colSpan === 2">
    @switch (config().type) {
      @case ('textarea') {
        <mat-form-field appearance="outline" subscriptSizing="dynamic" class="dyf__field">
          <mat-label>{{ config().label }}</mat-label>
          <textarea matInput [formControl]="control()" [placeholder]="config().placeholder ?? ''" rows="3"></textarea>
          @if (config().hint && !errorMessage()) {
            <mat-hint>{{ config().hint }}</mat-hint>
          }
          @if (errorMessage()) {
            <mat-error>{{ errorMessage() }}</mat-error>
          }
        </mat-form-field>
      }
      @case ('select') {
        <mat-form-field appearance="outline" subscriptSizing="dynamic" class="dyf__field">
          <mat-label>{{ config().label }}</mat-label>
          <mat-select [formControl]="control()" [panelClass]="selectPanelClass()" (openedChange)="onSelectOpenedChange($event)">
            @if (config().searchable) {
              <div class="dyf__select-search">
                <mat-icon class="dyf__select-search-icon">search</mat-icon>
                <input
                  #searchInput
                  type="text"
                  class="dyf__select-search-input"
                  placeholder="Search..."
                  [value]="optionSearchTerm()"
                  (click)="$event.stopPropagation()"
                  (keydown)="$event.stopPropagation()"
                  (input)="onOptionSearchInput($event)"
                />
              </div>
            }
            @for (opt of filteredOptions(); track optionValue(opt)) {
              <mat-option [value]="optionValue(opt)">{{ optionLabel(opt) }}</mat-option>
            }
            @if (config().searchable && filteredOptions().length === 0) {
              <div class="dyf__select-empty">No matches found.</div>
            }
          </mat-select>
          @if (config().hint && !errorMessage()) {
            <mat-hint>{{ config().hint }}</mat-hint>
          }
          @if (errorMessage()) {
            <mat-error>{{ errorMessage() }}</mat-error>
          }
        </mat-form-field>
      }
      @case ('combo') {
        <mat-form-field appearance="outline" subscriptSizing="dynamic" class="dyf__field">
          <mat-label>{{ config().label }}</mat-label>
          <input matInput [formControl]="control()" [matAutocomplete]="auto" [placeholder]="config().placeholder ?? 'Type or select from dropdown'" />
          <mat-autocomplete #auto="matAutocomplete" [class]="selectPanelClass()">
            @for (opt of resolvedOptions(); track optionValue(opt)) {
              <mat-option [value]="optionValue(opt)">{{ optionLabel(opt) }}</mat-option>
            }
          </mat-autocomplete>
          @if (config().hint && !errorMessage()) {
            <mat-hint>{{ config().hint }}</mat-hint>
          }
          @if (errorMessage()) {
            <mat-error>{{ errorMessage() }}</mat-error>
          }
        </mat-form-field>
      }
      @case ('rich-textarea') {
        <app-wwell-rich-textarea [config]="config()" [control]="control()" [selectPanelClass]="selectPanelClass()" />
      }
      @case ('toggle-group') {
        <div class="dyf__toggle">
          <span class="dyf__toggle-label">
            {{ config().label }}
            @if (config().validators?.required) {
              <span class="dyf__required">*</span>
            }
          </span>
          <mat-button-toggle-group class="dyf__toggle-group" [formControl]="control()">
            @for (opt of resolvedOptions(); track optionValue(opt)) {
              <mat-button-toggle [value]="optionValue(opt)">
                @if (optionIcon(opt); as icon) {
                  <mat-icon class="dyf__toggle-icon">{{ icon }}</mat-icon>
                }
                {{ optionLabel(opt) }}
              </mat-button-toggle>
            }
          </mat-button-toggle-group>
          @if (errorMessage()) {
            <div class="dyf__toggle-error">{{ errorMessage() }}</div>
          }
        </div>
      }
      @case ('file') {
        <div class="dyf__file">
          <span class="dyf__toggle-label">{{ config().label }}</span>
          <app-wwell-attachment-dropzone
            [hint]="config().placeholder ?? 'Click to attach file...'"
            (filesChange)="onFilesChange($event)"
          />
          @if (errorMessage()) {
            <div class="dyf__toggle-error">{{ errorMessage() }}</div>
          }
        </div>
      }
      @case ('number') {
        <!-- `type="number"` must be a static attribute, not `[type]`, so Angular's
             NumberValueAccessor selector (`input[type=number][formControl]`) actually
             matches at compile time and parses the value to a real `number` — a dynamic
             `[type]` binding is invisible to that (template-AST-based) directive match,
             silently falling back to the string-based DefaultValueAccessor instead. -->
        <mat-form-field appearance="outline" subscriptSizing="dynamic" class="dyf__field">
          <mat-label>{{ config().label }}</mat-label>
          <input matInput type="number" [formControl]="control()" [placeholder]="config().placeholder ?? ''" />
          @if (config().suffix) {
            <span matTextSuffix class="dyf__suffix">&nbsp;{{ config().suffix }}</span>
          }
          @if (config().hint && !errorMessage()) {
            <mat-hint>{{ config().hint }}</mat-hint>
          }
          @if (errorMessage()) {
            <mat-error>{{ errorMessage() }}</mat-error>
          }
        </mat-form-field>
      }
      @default {
        <mat-form-field appearance="outline" subscriptSizing="dynamic" class="dyf__field">
          <mat-label>{{ config().label }}</mat-label>
          <input matInput type="text" [formControl]="control()" [placeholder]="config().placeholder ?? ''" />
          @if (config().suffix) {
            <span matTextSuffix class="dyf__suffix">&nbsp;{{ config().suffix }}</span>
          }
          @if (config().hint && !errorMessage()) {
            <mat-hint>{{ config().hint }}</mat-hint>
          }
          @if (errorMessage()) {
            <mat-error>{{ errorMessage() }}</mat-error>
          }
        </mat-form-field>
      }
    }
  </div>
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/dynamic-form/dynamic-field/dynamic-field.component.ts

```ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, ElementRef, computed, input, signal, viewChild } from '@angular/core';
import { toObservable, toSignal } from '@angular/core/rxjs-interop';
import { FormControl, ReactiveFormsModule } from '@angular/forms';
import { MatAutocompleteModule } from '@angular/material/autocomplete';
import { MatButtonToggleModule } from '@angular/material/button-toggle';
import { MatFormFieldModule } from '@angular/material/form-field';
import { MatIconModule } from '@angular/material/icon';
import { MatInputModule } from '@angular/material/input';
import { MatSelectModule } from '@angular/material/select';
import { switchMap, startWith } from 'rxjs';
import { WwellAttachmentDropzoneComponent } from '../../wwell-attachment-dropzone/wwell-attachment-dropzone.component';
import { WwellRichTextareaComponent } from '../rich-textarea/wwell-rich-textarea.component';
import {
  WwellFieldConfig,
  resolveFieldOptions,
  wwellFieldOptionIcon,
  wwellFieldOptionLabel,
  wwellFieldOptionValue,
} from '../wwell-dynamic-form.types';

export type WwellDynamicFieldMode = 'edit' | 'review';

@Component({
  selector: 'app-dynamic-field',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [
    ReactiveFormsModule,
    MatFormFieldModule,
    MatInputModule,
    MatSelectModule,
    MatButtonToggleModule,
    MatAutocompleteModule,
    MatIconModule,
    WwellAttachmentDropzoneComponent,
    WwellRichTextareaComponent,
  ],
  templateUrl: './dynamic-field.component.html',
  styleUrl: './dynamic-field.component.scss',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class DynamicFieldComponent {
  readonly config = input.required<WwellFieldConfig>();
  readonly control = input.required<FormControl>();
  readonly mode = input<WwellDynamicFieldMode>('edit');
  /** Class applied to this field's portaled select/autocomplete overlay panel, so a host page can theme it. */
  readonly selectPanelClass = input('');

  protected readonly optionLabel = wwellFieldOptionLabel;
  protected readonly optionValue = wwellFieldOptionValue;
  protected readonly optionIcon = wwellFieldOptionIcon;

  /** Resolves the config's `options` (static array or live Signal) once per change — every
   *  options `@for` loop below reads this instead of `config().options` directly. */
  protected readonly resolvedOptions = computed(() => resolveFieldOptions(this.config()));

  /** Backs the filter input a `searchable: true` 'select' field projects into its own mat-select
   *  panel (see the template) — mat-select has no built-in search. Stays '' for every other
   *  field, since nothing renders an input to change it, so `filteredOptions` below is then just
   *  `resolvedOptions()` unfiltered. */
  protected readonly optionSearchTerm = signal('');

  /** The searchable-select panel's filter `<input>` — queried instead of using the `autofocus`
   *  attribute (flagged by @angular-eslint/template/no-autofocus and unreliable for content
   *  that mounts into a CDK overlay after the panel opens). */
  private readonly searchInput = viewChild<ElementRef<HTMLInputElement>>('searchInput');

  protected readonly filteredOptions = computed(() => {
    const term = this.optionSearchTerm().trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!term) return this.resolvedOptions();
    return this.resolvedOptions().filter(opt => this.optionLabel(opt).toLowerCase().includes(term));
  });

  /** Clears the filter on close so reopening the panel always starts from the full list instead
   *  of whatever was last typed. On open, focuses the filter input once the overlay panel has
   *  attached — a plain template `autofocus` fires before that element exists in the DOM. */
  protected onSelectOpenedChange(opened: boolean): void {
    if (!opened) {
      this.optionSearchTerm.set('');
      return;
    }
    setTimeout(() => this.searchInput()?.nativeElement.focus());
  }

  protected onOptionSearchInput(event: Event): void {
    this.optionSearchTerm.set((event.target as HTMLInputElement).value);
  }

  /**
   * `control()` is a Signal input, but the bound FormControl's own `.value` mutates in place
   * on every keystroke and is never itself signal-tracked — reading it directly inside a
   * `computed()` (as `reviewValue` below used to) only evaluates once, at first read, and then
   * freezes forever: the Attachments & Review step's tiles kept showing '—' even after the
   * matching field was filled in on an earlier step. Bridging through `valueChanges` (and
   * re-subscribing whenever a *different* control is bound) keeps this genuinely reactive.
   */
  // No `initialValue`/`requireSync`: reading `this.control()` synchronously in a field
  // initializer (for an `initialValue`) fails the compiler's NG8118 check — inputs aren't bound
  // yet at that point — and `toObservable` doesn't emit synchronously either (its effect is
  // scheduled, not immediate), so `requireSync` throws NG0601 at construction instead. Left
  // without either, `liveValue()` is simply `undefined` for the first tick, which `reviewValue`
  // below already treats the same as an empty value.
  private readonly liveValue = toSignal(
    toObservable(this.control).pipe(switchMap(control => control.valueChanges.pipe(startWith(control.value)))),
  );

  protected readonly reviewValue = computed(() => {
    const value = this.liveValue();
    const cfg = this.config();

    if (cfg.type === 'file') {
      const files = value as File[] | null;
      return files?.length ? `${files.length} file(s) attached` : '—';
    }
    if (cfg.type === 'select' || cfg.type === 'toggle-group') {
      const match = this.resolvedOptions().find(opt => wwellFieldOptionValue(opt) === value);
      return match ? wwellFieldOptionLabel(match) : (value || '—');
    }
    return value === null || value === undefined || value === '' ? '—' : String(value);
  });

  protected readonly isReviewValueEmpty = computed(() => this.reviewValue() === '—');

  protected onFilesChange(files: File[]): void {
    this.control().setValue(files);
    this.control().markAsTouched();
  }

  protected errorMessage(): string | null {
    const control = this.control();
    if (!control.touched || control.valid) return null;
    if (control.hasError('required')) return `${this.config().label} is required`;
    if (control.hasError('pattern')) return `${this.config().label} format is invalid`;
    if (control.hasError('min')) return `${this.config().label} is below the minimum`;
    return 'Invalid value';
  }
}
```

## [A] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/dynamic-form/rich-textarea/wwell-rich-textarea.component.html

```html
<div class="wrt" [class.wrt--disabled]="control().disabled">
  <div class="wrt__head">
    <span class="wrt__label">
      {{ config().label }}
      @if (config().validators?.required) {
        <span class="wrt__required">*</span>
      }
    </span>
  </div>

  <div class="wrt__field">
    @if (hasOptions()) {
      <button
        type="button"
        class="wrt__dropdown-btn"
        aria-label="Select from dropdown"
        [disabled]="control().disabled"
        [matMenuTriggerFor]="optionsMenu"
        [matMenuTriggerRestoreFocus]="false"
      >
        <mat-icon>arrow_drop_down</mat-icon>
      </button>
      <mat-menu #optionsMenu="matMenu" [class]="selectPanelClass()">
        <div class="wrt__option-list">
          @for (opt of resolvedOptions(); track optionValue(opt)) {
            <mat-checkbox
              class="wrt__option"
              [checked]="isOptionChecked(opt)"
              (change)="toggleOption(opt, $event)"
              (click)="$event.stopPropagation()"
            >
              {{ optionLabel(opt) }}
            </mat-checkbox>
          }
        </div>
      </mat-menu>
    }

    <div class="wrt__box-scroll">
      <div
        #box
        class="wrt__box"
        [class.wrt__box--no-dropdown]="!hasOptions()"
        [attr.contenteditable]="control().disabled ? null : true"
        [attr.data-placeholder]="config().placeholder ?? 'Type or select from dropdown'"
        (input)="onBoxInput()"
      ></div>

      @if (currentLines().length > 0) {
        <div class="wrt__line-removers">
          @for (line of currentLines(); track $index) {
            <button
              type="button"
              class="wrt__line-remove"
              [attr.aria-label]="'Remove: ' + line"
              title="Remove this line"
              [disabled]="control().disabled"
              (click)="removeLine($index)"
            >
              <mat-icon aria-hidden="true">delete</mat-icon>
            </button>
          }
        </div>
      }
    </div>
  </div>

  @if (config().hint) {
    <p class="wrt__hint">{{ config().hint }}</p>
  }
</div>
```

## [A] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/dynamic-form/rich-textarea/wwell-rich-textarea.component.scss

```scss
@use '../../wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;

$navy: tokens.$navy-deep;
$muted: tokens.$muted;
$border: tokens.$border;
$accent: tokens.$accent;

:host {
  display: block;
  width: 100%;
}

.wrt {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.35rem;
  width: 100%;
}

.wrt__head {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}

.wrt__label {
  font-size: 0.76rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
  color: #94a3b8;
}

.wrt__required {
  margin-left: 0.15rem;
  color: #dc2626;
}

/* Bordered "▼ | text" control — the dropdown button and the writable box share one outline so
 * they read as a single field, matching the reference screen's combo-box chrome. Plain white
 * throughout (not just on focus) per spec — only the border changes color on focus. */
.wrt__field {
  display: flex;
  align-items: stretch;
  border: 1.5px solid $border;
  border-radius: 8px;
  background: #fff;
  overflow: hidden;
  transition:
    box-shadow 180ms ease,
    border-color 180ms ease;

  &:focus-within {
    border-color: $accent;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba($accent, 0.14);
  }
}

.wrt__dropdown-btn {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 32px;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  border: none;
  border-right: 1.5px solid $border;
  background: #fff;
  color: $muted;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: color 150ms ease;

  mat-icon {
    width: 18px;
    height: 18px;
    font-size: 18px;
  }

  &:hover:not(:disabled) {
    color: $accent;
  }

  &:disabled {
    cursor: not-allowed;
    opacity: 0.5;
  }
}

/* Checkbox list projected into the dropdown's portaled mat-menu panel — Angular's emulated
 * encapsulation stamps this component's scope attribute onto projected content itself, not onto
 * whatever element ends up hosting it, so these rules reach it even though MatMenu portals the
 * whole panel out to <body>. `<mat-menu>`'s own panel template has an unconditional
 * `(click)="closed.emit('click')"` on its root element (see @angular/material/menu) — ANY click
 * anywhere inside the panel bubbles up and closes it, not just a `mat-menu-item` click. The
 * template's `(click)="$event.stopPropagation()"` on each `<mat-checkbox>` is what stops that
 * bubble, so checking several options in one open actually keeps the panel open until a real
 * outside click (or Escape) closes it. */
.wrt__option-list {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 2px;
  padding: 0.35rem 0.85rem;
  max-height: 16rem;
  overflow-y: auto;
}

.wrt__option {
  font-size: 0.88rem;

  ::ng-deep .mdc-label {
    color: $navy;
  }
}

/* Wraps the writable box and its per-line remove-icon column in one shared scroll region — a
 * single `overflow-y` here (not one on each side separately) is what keeps the Nth icon lined up
 * with the Nth text line while scrolling a box with many lines; two independently-scrolling
 * siblings would drift apart the moment either one scrolled.
 *
 * `resize: vertical` puts a drag handle on this element (not the contenteditable directly) so the
 * box and its remove-icon column always resize together, staying the same height. `overflow: auto`
 * is required for `resize` to take effect at all — it's already needed here for the scrolling
 * behavior above. No `max-height`: a resize handle capped by a max-height can't actually resize
 * past it, which would defeat the point for anyone with a lot of text to work with. */
.wrt__box-scroll {
  display: flex;
  align-items: stretch;
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  // "Big" per spec — enough for several lines of remarks to be visible at once, not just a
  // single-line-tall combo input pretending to be a textarea.
  min-height: 7rem;
  overflow: auto;
  resize: vertical;
}

.wrt__box {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  padding: 0.5rem 0.75rem;
  font-size: 0.92rem;
  line-height: 1.4;
  color: $navy;
  outline: none;

  &--no-dropdown {
    padding-left: 0.75rem;
  }

  div {
    min-height: 1.4em;
  }

  &:empty::before {
    content: attr(data-placeholder);
    color: #94a3b8;
    pointer-events: none;
  }

  &[contenteditable='false'] {
    color: $muted;
    cursor: not-allowed;
  }
}

/* One remove button per current line, in the same top-to-bottom order — see
 * `WwellRichTextareaComponent.currentLines`. Lives outside the contenteditable box entirely
 * (never injected into its DOM) so removing a line can never corrupt `innerText` extraction or
 * fight the caret while the user is mid-keystroke elsewhere in the box. */
.wrt__line-removers {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  flex-shrink: 0;
  padding: 0.5rem 0.4rem 0.5rem 0;
  font-size: 0.92rem;
}

$danger: #dc2626;
$danger-hover: #b91c1c;

.wrt__line-remove {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  width: 1.6em;
  min-height: 1.4em;
  padding: 0;
  border: none;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: transparent;
  // Red always, not just on hover — a delete affordance that needs a hover to even read as
  // destructive is easy to miss until it's too late.
  color: $danger;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition:
    background-color 150ms ease,
    color 150ms ease;

  mat-icon {
    width: 15px;
    height: 15px;
    font-size: 15px;
  }

  &:hover:not(:disabled) {
    background: rgba($danger, 0.12);
    color: $danger-hover;
  }

  &:disabled {
    cursor: not-allowed;
    opacity: 0.4;
  }
}

.wrt--disabled .wrt__field {
  background: rgba($muted, 0.08);
}

.wrt__hint {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 0.76rem;
  color: $muted;
}
```

## [A] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/dynamic-form/rich-textarea/wwell-rich-textarea.component.ts

```ts
import { AfterViewInit, ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component, ElementRef, OnDestroy, computed, input, signal, viewChild } from '@angular/core';
import { FormControl } from '@angular/forms';
import { MatCheckboxChange, MatCheckboxModule } from '@angular/material/checkbox';
import { MatIconModule } from '@angular/material/icon';
import { MatMenuModule } from '@angular/material/menu';
import { Subscription } from 'rxjs';
import {
  WwellFieldConfig,
  WwellFieldOption,
  resolveFieldOptions,
  wwellFieldOptionLabel,
  wwellFieldOptionValue,
} from '../wwell-dynamic-form.types';

/**
 * "Dropdown + big textarea" control for the IR Program Details remarks fields (Acidization,
 * Transmission, Logging, Mud Logging, Sampling, Flow Test, Development/Testing) — several of
 * these carry long, multi-line legacy remarks (see wwell-ir-legacy-html.util.ts), too much for the
 * single-line `combo` autocomplete input the rest of the dynamic-form engine uses.
 *
 * The dropdown is a checkbox list, not a single-pick select: any number of the field's canned
 * options can be checked at once, and each checked option becomes its own line in the box below
 * (see `toggleOption`) — unchecking one removes exactly that line. A box line that doesn't match
 * any option's label (free-typed text) is left alone either way. Checked state is derived live
 * from the box's own content (`checkedLabels`), not tracked separately, so typing a line that
 * happens to match an option's label also shows it checked, and deleting that line unchecks it.
 *
 * The `FormControl<string>` this binds to always holds plain text with real `\n` line breaks —
 * never markup. Deliberately no rich-formatting toolbar (bold/italic/font-size were all tried and
 * dropped) — the IR "no HTML in storage" fix (wwell-ir-legacy-html.util.ts) intentionally keeps
 * these columns plain-text-only, so there was never anything for formatting to actually persist.
 *
 * Not a `ControlValueAccessor` — `DynamicFieldComponent` (this control's only caller) already
 * hands every field its own live `FormControl` instance directly rather than binding through
 * `formControlName`/`[formControl]`, so this only needs to read/write that instance's `.value`
 * and listen to its `valueChanges`, not implement the full CVA contract.
 */
@Component({
  selector: 'app-wwell-rich-textarea',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MatIconModule, MatMenuModule, MatCheckboxModule],
  templateUrl: './wwell-rich-textarea.component.html',
  styleUrl: './wwell-rich-textarea.component.scss',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class WwellRichTextareaComponent implements AfterViewInit, OnDestroy {
  readonly config = input.required<WwellFieldConfig>();
  readonly control = input.required<FormControl>();
  /** Class applied to the options dropdown's portaled mat-menu panel, so a host page can theme it — mirrors DynamicFieldComponent's own `selectPanelClass`. */
  readonly selectPanelClass = input('');

  protected readonly optionLabel = wwellFieldOptionLabel;
  protected readonly optionValue = wwellFieldOptionValue;
  protected readonly resolvedOptions = computed(() => resolveFieldOptions(this.config()));
  protected readonly hasOptions = computed(() => this.resolvedOptions().length > 0);

  private readonly boxRef = viewChild<ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>>('box');
  private valueChangesSubscription?: Subscription;
  /** Set immediately before this component pushes its own edit into the control, so the
   *  `valueChanges` echo that causes doesn't get rebuilt back into the DOM — rebuilding on every
   *  keystroke would reset the caret to the start of the box on every character typed. */
  private isOwnEdit = false;

  /** Mirrors the control's current plain-text value as a signal purely so `checkedLabels` (and
   *  therefore each checkbox's checked state) stays reactive — kept in sync on every value change
   *  regardless of source (typed, or a checkbox toggle), unlike `isOwnEdit` which only gates
   *  whether the DOM box itself needs rebuilding. */
  private readonly boxText = signal('');
  private readonly checkedLabels = computed(() => new Set(this.linesOf(this.boxText())));
  /** Backs the remove-icon column — one button per current line, same order, always derived from
   *  `boxText` so it (and the checkboxes above, via `checkedLabels`) never drift out of sync with
   *  whatever the box actually contains, regardless of whether a line arrived by checking an
   *  option, typing directly, or a previous remove-icon click. */
  protected readonly currentLines = computed(() => this.linesOf(this.boxText()));

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    // Normalizes Enter-key behavior across browsers to one `<div>` per line (Chrome/Edge already
    // default to this; Firefox defaults to `<br>`-separated lines within a single block instead)
    // — renderIntoBox/extractPlainText below both assume "one line == one `<div>`".
    document.execCommand('defaultParagraphSeparator', false, 'div');
    const initial = this.control().value ?? '';
    this.boxText.set(initial);
    this.renderIntoBox(initial);
    this.valueChangesSubscription = this.control().valueChanges.subscribe((value: string) => {
      this.boxText.set(value ?? '');
      if (this.isOwnEdit) {
        this.isOwnEdit = false;
        return;
      }
      this.renderIntoBox(value ?? '');
    });
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.valueChangesSubscription?.unsubscribe();
  }

  /** A dropdown checkbox flipping on appends that option's label as a new line (unless it's
   *  already present); flipping off removes exactly that line. Multiple options can be checked at
   *  once — each becomes its own line, always appended, never inserted mid-text. */
  protected toggleOption(option: string | WwellFieldOption, event: MatCheckboxChange): void {
    const label = this.optionLabel(option);
    const lines = this.linesOf(this.extractPlainText());
    const nextLines = event.checked
      ? (lines.includes(label) ? lines : [...lines, label])
      : lines.filter(line => line !== label);
    this.renderIntoBox(nextLines.join('\n'));
    this.commitFromBox();
  }

  protected isOptionChecked(option: string | WwellFieldOption): boolean {
    return this.checkedLabels().has(this.optionLabel(option));
  }

  /** The remove-icon column's click handler — drops one line by position (not by text, so two
   *  identical lines can be removed independently) and commits, which flows straight back through
   *  `boxText`/`checkedLabels` and unchecks that option's checkbox if the removed line matched one. */
  protected removeLine(index: number): void {
    const lines = this.linesOf(this.boxText());
    lines.splice(index, 1);
    this.renderIntoBox(lines.join('\n'));
    this.commitFromBox();
  }

  protected onBoxInput(): void {
    this.commitFromBox();
  }

  private commitFromBox(): void {
    const text = this.extractPlainText();
    this.isOwnEdit = true;
    this.boxText.set(text);
    this.control().setValue(text);
    this.control().markAsTouched();
  }

  /** Deliberately does NOT drop blank lines — `currentLines()` (backing the remove-icon column)
   *  needs to stay index-aligned 1:1 with `renderIntoBox`'s one-`<div>`-per-line output, blank
   *  lines included, or a blank line in the middle would leave the icon column one short and every
   *  icon after it would line up with the wrong row. A genuinely empty box still yields `[]` here
   *  (the `text ? ... : []` guard), which is what keeps the icon column hidden when there's really
   *  nothing in the box — see the template's `@if (currentLines().length > 0)`. */
  private linesOf(text: string): string[] {
    return text ? text.split('\n') : [];
  }

  /** `innerText` (not `textContent`) — reflects the box's actual rendered lines as `\n`-joined
   *  text, matching how `renderIntoBox` below wrote them in (one `<div>` per line). */
  private extractPlainText(): string {
    const text = this.boxRef()?.nativeElement.innerText ?? '';
    return text.replace(/\n+$/, '');
  }

  /** Rebuilds the box's content from plain text — one `<div>` per line, an empty line rendered as
   *  `<div><br></div>` so it keeps its line height (a truly empty `<div>` collapses to zero height
   *  in most browsers). Leaves the box with no children at all for an empty value, so the
   *  `:empty::before` placeholder in the stylesheet actually shows. */
  private renderIntoBox(text: string): void {
    const box = this.boxRef()?.nativeElement;
    if (!box) return;
    box.textContent = '';
    if (!text) return;

    text.split('\n').forEach(line => {
      const row = document.createElement('div');
      if (line) row.textContent = line;
      else row.appendChild(document.createElement('br'));
      box.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/shared/dynamic-form/wwell-dynamic-form.types.ts

```ts
/** Schema vocabulary for the wwell-planning dynamic form engine. Add a field = edit a schema array, not a template. */

import type { Signal } from '@angular/core';

export type WwellFieldControlType =
  | 'text'
  | 'number'
  | 'textarea'
  | 'select'
  | 'combo'
  | 'rich-textarea'
  | 'toggle-group'
  | 'file';

export interface WwellFieldOption {
  readonly value: string;
  readonly label: string;
  /** Material icon ligature name — used by 'toggle-group' only. */
  readonly icon?: string;
}

export interface WwellFieldValidatorConfig {
  readonly required?: boolean;
  readonly pattern?: RegExp;
  readonly min?: number;
}

/**
 * Field is only shown (and its control enabled) while a sibling control in the same section
 * matches this condition. Exactly one of `equals` | `oneOf` | `notOneOf` should be set:
 *  - `equals`: sibling value === this value
 *  - `oneOf`: sibling value is one of this set (e.g. a client tier)
 *  - `notOneOf`: sibling value is absent from this set — the fallback for values outside every
 *    named tier (e.g. a client not yet assigned to a tier keeps the default field set)
 */
export interface WwellFieldVisibilityCondition {
  readonly field: string;
  readonly equals?: string;
  readonly oneOf?: readonly string[];
  readonly notOneOf?: readonly string[];
}

export interface WwellFieldConfig {
  readonly key: string;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly type: WwellFieldControlType;
  readonly placeholder?: string;
  readonly hint?: string;
  readonly suffix?: string;
  /** Used by 'select' | 'combo' | 'toggle-group'. Plain strings are shown as their own label.
   *  A Signal is resolved lazily via `resolveFieldOptions` — for options that come from a store
   *  (e.g. the IR wizard's "Link to Request" picker) rather than being known at schema-definition
   *  time (the schema array itself is a static, module-level constant with no injector). */
  readonly options?: readonly (string | WwellFieldOption)[] | Signal<readonly (string | WwellFieldOption)[]>;
  readonly validators?: WwellFieldValidatorConfig;
  readonly colSpan?: 1 | 2;
  readonly visibleWhen?: WwellFieldVisibilityCondition;
  /** Overrides the type-based empty default (e.g. pre-selecting a toggle-group option). */
  readonly defaultValue?: string | number;
  /** 'select' only — adds a filter input inside the option panel, narrowing `options` as the user
   *  types. For a picker backed by a long or live-fetched list (e.g. the IR wizard's "Link to
   *  Request", backed by every well eligible for IR initiation) where scrolling an unfiltered
   *  mat-select is impractical; the short, hand-picked lists most 'select' fields use don't need it. */
  readonly searchable?: boolean;
  /** Disables the control — for values derived elsewhere (e.g. a server-computed metric) rather
   *  than typed in directly. Pair with a `placeholder` like 'auto' to signal that visually. */
  readonly readonly?: boolean;
}

export interface WwellRepeatableRowConfig {
  readonly key: string;
  readonly addLabel: string;
  readonly rowFields: readonly WwellFieldConfig[];
  /** How many empty rows to seed the array with on load. */
  readonly minRows?: number;
  /** If set, the array itself is invalid until it holds at least this many rows — an empty array is not "complete" just because it has no invalid children. */
  readonly minRequiredRows?: number;
}

export interface WwellFormSectionConfig {
  readonly key: string;
  readonly title: string | null;
  /** Mutually exclusive with `repeatable`. */
  readonly fields?: readonly WwellFieldConfig[];
  readonly repeatable?: WwellRepeatableRowConfig;
}

export interface WwellFormStepConfig {
  readonly key: string;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly sections: readonly WwellFormSectionConfig[];
}

export function wwellFieldOptionLabel(option: string | WwellFieldOption): string {
  return typeof option === 'string' ? option : option.label;
}

export function wwellFieldOptionValue(option: string | WwellFieldOption): string {
  return typeof option === 'string' ? option : option.value;
}

export function wwellFieldOptionIcon(option: string | WwellFieldOption): string | undefined {
  return typeof option === 'string' ? undefined : option.icon;
}

/** Unwraps a field's `options` (static array or live Signal) into the array consumers iterate. */
export function resolveFieldOptions(field: WwellFieldConfig): readonly (string | WwellFieldOption)[] {
  const options = field.options;
  if (!options) return [];
  return typeof options === 'function' ? options() : options;
}
```

```ts
 * collect: Tier 1 clients supply target depth/aquifer at intake; Tier 2 clients don't (that data
 * comes later) but do supply an as-drilled final coordinate. A client outside both named tiers
 * (e.g. BI-15) falls back to the Tier 1 field set.
```

```ts
import { TestBed } from '@angular/core/testing';
import { of, throwError } from 'rxjs';
import { CreateIntegratedRequirementPayload } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS } from '@models/wwell-planning/wwell-planning-loading.constants';
import { buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload } from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-draft.util';
import { WwellIrBulkSavePayload } from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-payload.model';
import { WwellIrService } from '../wwell-ir.service';
import { WwellIrStore } from './wwell-ir.store';

const serviceMock = {
  createIr: jest.fn(),
  getIrDetailBlocks: jest.fn(),
};

function buildPayload(): CreateIntegratedRequirementPayload {
  return {
    status: 'in-progress',
    requestReference: {
      linkedRequestId: null,
      client: 'BI-12',
      supportingBusiness: 'Agriculture',
      wellName: 'HAIL-500',
      targetAquifer: 'Saq',
      targetDepthFt: 510,
      surfaceElevationFtAsl: 980,
      coordinates: '27.5114°N, 41.7208°E',
    },
    formationTops: [],
    casing: [],
    programDetails: {
      completion: 'Screened',
      openHoleSizeIn: null,
      acidization: '',
      transmission: '',
      developmentTesting: { logging: '', mudLogging: '', sampling: '', flowTest: '', remarks: '' },
    },
    contacts: { geologistPrimary: 'Eng. A. Al-Mutairi', geologistSecondary: null },
    offsetWells: [],
    offsetWellsRemarks: null,
    hydrogeology: { aquifer: 'Saq', estimatedTdsPpm: 1200, h2sPpm: 5, estimatedPumpDepthFt: 150 },
    testProgram: { testType: 'pump-test', estimatedRateGpm: 350, estimatedWaterLevelFt: 95 },
  };
}

function buildIrBulkPayload(): WwellIrBulkSavePayload {
  return [
    {
      table: 'exad_gwd_ir_header',
      data: [
        {
          action: 'INSERT',
          ir_seq: null,
          prewap_seq: null,
          ltr_no: null,
          screen: '',
          sampling: null,
          logging: null,
          tops_remarks: null,
          publish_dt: null,
          publish_flg: 'N',
          curr_flg: 'Y',
          publish_by: null,
          cont_geologist: 101,
          sub_cont_geologist: null,
          water_remarks: null,
          gen_remarks: null,
          completion: 'Screened',
          cmpl_type: null,
          acidization: null,
          trns_type: null,
          dt_rmks: null,
          mud_rmks: null,
          test_type: 'pump-test',
          flow_rmks: null,
        },
      ],
    },
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_casing', data: null },
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_hydrogeology', data: null },
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_tops', data: null },
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_water', data: null },
  ];
}

describe('WwellIrStore - createIr', () => {
  let store: InstanceType<typeof WwellIrStore>;

  beforeEach(() => {
    jest.clearAllMocks();
    jest.useFakeTimers();
    // createIr no longer trusts a client-side echo (see its own doc comment in WwellIrStore) — an
    // empty detail response here means "nothing extra to merge," so `irs()` ends up exactly what
    // buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload alone would build, matching most of these assertions.
    serviceMock.getIrDetailBlocks.mockReturnValue(of([]));
    TestBed.configureTestingModule({
      providers: [WwellIrStore, { provide: WwellIrService, useValue: serviceMock }],
    });
    store = TestBed.inject(WwellIrStore);
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    jest.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('inserts the confirmed record — passes attachment names through, waits out the real follow-up GET', () => {
    const attachmentNames = ['map.pdf'];
    serviceMock.createIr.mockReturnValue(of(9001));

    store.createIr(buildPayload(), buildIrBulkPayload(), attachmentNames);
    // createIr's request is wrapped in delay(WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS) — see WwellIrStore — so its own
    // "creatingIr" flag stays true for at least that long even against this instant mock response.
    jest.advanceTimersByTime(WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS);

    expect(serviceMock.createIr).toHaveBeenCalledWith(buildIrBulkPayload());
    expect(serviceMock.getIrDetailBlocks).toHaveBeenCalledWith(9001);
    expect(store.irs()).toEqual([
      buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload(buildPayload(), { id: 'IR-9001', code: 'IR-9001', irSeq: 9001 }, attachmentNames),
    ]);
    expect(store.creatingIr()).toBe(false);
    expect(store.createIrError()).toBeNull();
  });

  it('defaults attachment names to an empty array when omitted', () => {
    serviceMock.createIr.mockReturnValue(of(9001));

    store.createIr(buildPayload(), buildIrBulkPayload());
    jest.advanceTimersByTime(WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS);

    expect(store.irs()[0].attachmentNames).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('sets createIrError on failure and clears the creating flag', () => {
    serviceMock.createIr.mockReturnValue(throwError(() => new Error('Network down')));

    store.createIr(buildPayload(), buildIrBulkPayload());

    // delay() only defers next/complete notifications, not errors — see WwellIrStore's own note
    // on createIr — so the error path settles synchronously, with no timer to advance.
    expect(store.creatingIr()).toBe(false);
    expect(store.createIrError()).toBe('Network down');
    expect(serviceMock.getIrDetailBlocks).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('surfaces a distinct error and leaves irs untouched when the save succeeds but the follow-up GET fails', () => {
    serviceMock.createIr.mockReturnValue(of(9001));
    serviceMock.getIrDetailBlocks.mockReturnValue(throwError(() => new Error('timeout')));

    store.createIr(buildPayload(), buildIrBulkPayload());
    jest.advanceTimersByTime(WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS);

    expect(store.irs()).toEqual([]);
    expect(store.creatingIr()).toBe(false);
    // The backend did confirm this ir_seq, so it's still remembered — a resave must UPDATE, not
    // re-INSERT under a new row, even though `irs` couldn't be refreshed this time.
    expect(store.draftIrSeq()).toBe(9001);
    expect(store.createIrError()).toContain('refresh');
  });

  it('remembers the returned ir_seq — WwellIrComponent.createNewIr() reads this to select the just-created draft', () => {
    serviceMock.createIr.mockReturnValue(of(9001));

    store.createIr(buildPayload(), buildIrBulkPayload());
    jest.advanceTimersByTime(WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS);

    expect(store.draftIrSeq()).toBe(9001);
  });
});
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/store/wwell-ir.store.ts

```ts
import { computed, inject } from '@angular/core';
import { HttpErrorResponse } from '@angular/common/http';
import { patchState, signalStore, withComputed, withMethods, withState } from '@ngrx/signals';
import { rxMethod } from '@ngrx/signals/rxjs-interop';
import { tapResponse } from '@ngrx/operators';
import { catchError, delay, map, of, pipe, switchMap, tap } from 'rxjs';
import { CreateIntegratedRequirementPayload, IntegratedRequirement } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import {
  WWELL_DELETE_DELAY_MS,
  WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS,
  WWELL_UPDATE_DELAY_MS,
} from '@models/wwell-planning/wwell-planning-loading.constants';
import { mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement } from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-detail-response.mapper';
import { buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload } from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-draft.util';
import { WwellIrBulkSavePayload } from '../wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-payload.model';
import { WwellIrService } from '../wwell-ir.service';
import { WwellIrStoreState, initialWwellIrState } from './wwell-ir.state';

/** Prefers the backend's own `{ message }` (see ApiResponse<T> — every exad_agwa endpoint here
 *  returns that envelope on both success and error) over Angular's generic "Http failure
 *  response for ..." text, so a specific backend validation error (e.g. an Oracle constraint
 *  message) reaches the user instead of a vague fallback. */
function errorMessage(err: unknown, fallback: string): string {
  if (err instanceof HttpErrorResponse) {
    const body: unknown = err.error;
    const backendMessage = body && typeof body === 'object' ? (body as { message?: unknown }).message : undefined;
    if (typeof backendMessage === 'string' && backendMessage) return backendMessage;
    return err.message || fallback;
  }
  return err instanceof Error ? err.message : fallback;
}

/** A repeat "Save as Draft" on the same IR reuses its ir_seq (see wwell-ir-payload.mapper.ts), so
 *  the response describes the *same* record, not a new one — replace it in place instead of
 *  prepending a duplicate. Falls back to prepend for a genuinely new IR (irSeq not seen yet). */
function upsertByIrSeq(list: readonly IntegratedRequirement[], created: IntegratedRequirement): IntegratedRequirement[] {
  const existingIndex = created.irSeq != null ? list.findIndex(item => item.irSeq === created.irSeq) : -1;
  if (existingIndex === -1) return [created, ...list];
  // A brand-new record's `created` (see createIr's own buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload
  // fallback) has no way to know the record's real approval chain (that's a separate, still-mocked
  // endpoint; see loadIrApprovalTimeline), so it always starts a fresh "just initiated" chain.
  // Harmless for a first-time INSERT (nothing to preserve yet), but would otherwise wipe out an
  // already-in-progress/approved record's real chain on every subsequent save. Keep whichever
  // chain this record already had.
  return list.map((item, index) => (index === existingIndex ? { ...created, chain: item.chain } : item));
}

export const WwellIrStore = signalStore(
  withState<WwellIrStoreState>(initialWwellIrState),

  withComputed(({ irsLoading, linkedRequestWells, linkedRequestWellsLoading, irsError, linkedRequestWellsError, createIrError, updateIrStatusError, irDetailError, irApprovalTimelineError, deleteIrError }) => ({
    isLoading: computed(() => irsLoading() || linkedRequestWellsLoading()),
    /** Trimmed {value,label} view of linkedRequestWells for the "Link to Request" picker itself —
     *  see wwell-ir-editor.component.ts's `applyLinkedRequestWell` for where the full record
     *  (looked up from linkedRequestWells by this same value) drives the auto-populate. */
    linkedRequestWellOptions: computed(() => linkedRequestWells().map(well => ({ value: String(well.prewapSeq), label: well.wellName }))),
    activeErrors: computed(() =>
      [irsError(), linkedRequestWellsError(), createIrError(), updateIrStatusError(), irDetailError(), irApprovalTimelineError(), deleteIrError()].filter(
        (message): message is string => message !== null,
      ),
    ),
    hasError: computed(
      () =>
        irsError() !== null ||
        linkedRequestWellsError() !== null ||
        createIrError() !== null ||
        updateIrStatusError() !== null ||
        irDetailError() !== null ||
        irApprovalTimelineError() !== null ||
        deleteIrError() !== null,
    ),
  })),

  withMethods((store, svc = inject(WwellIrService)) => {
    const loadIrs = rxMethod<void>(
      pipe(
        tap(() => patchState(store, { irsLoading: true, irsError: null })),
        switchMap(() =>
          svc.getIrs().pipe(
            tapResponse({
              next: irs => patchState(store, { irs, irsLoading: false }),
              error: (err: unknown) => patchState(store, { irsLoading: false, irsError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to load integrated requirements') }),
            }),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    const loadLinkedRequestWells = rxMethod<void>(
      pipe(
        tap(() => {
          console.log('[IR:link] loadLinkedRequestWells — fetching GET /wells/eligible-for-ir');
          patchState(store, { linkedRequestWellsLoading: true, linkedRequestWellsError: null });
        }),
        switchMap(() =>
          svc.getEligibleWellsForIr().pipe(
            tapResponse({
              next: linkedRequestWells => {
                console.log('[IR:link] loadLinkedRequestWells — response received', { count: linkedRequestWells.length, linkedRequestWells });
                patchState(store, { linkedRequestWells, linkedRequestWellsLoading: false });
              },
              error: (err: unknown) => {
                console.log('[IR:link] loadLinkedRequestWells — request failed', err);
                patchState(store, {
                  linkedRequestWellsLoading: false,
                  linkedRequestWellsError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to load wells eligible for IR initiation'),
                });
              },
            }),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    // Keyed by ir_seq (the real primary key once an IR is saved) — switchMap cancels a still-in-
    // flight fetch for a previously-selected row if the user picks another one before it resolves,
    // so a slow first response can never land after (and clobber) a faster later selection.
    const loadIrDetail = rxMethod<number>(
      pipe(
        tap(() => patchState(store, { irDetailLoading: true, irDetailError: null })),
        switchMap(irSeq =>
          svc.getIrDetailBlocks(irSeq).pipe(
            tapResponse({
              next: blocks => {
                const existing = store.irs().find(item => item.irSeq === irSeq);
                // Nothing to merge onto if the summary row isn't loaded (shouldn't happen from the
                // normal select-a-row flow) — just clear the loading flag and leave state as-is.
                if (!existing) {
                  patchState(store, { irDetailLoading: false });
                  return;
                }
                const merged = mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement(existing, blocks);
                patchState(store, { irs: upsertByIrSeq(store.irs(), merged), irDetailLoading: false });
              },
              error: (err: unknown) =>
                patchState(store, { irDetailLoading: false, irDetailError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to load integrated requirement detail') }),
            }),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    // Separate call from loadIrDetail above — mirrors the future real API shape (approval process
    // gets its own endpoint), mocked client-side in the meantime. Same switchMap-cancels-a-stale-
    // fetch reasoning applies when the selection changes quickly.
    const loadIrApprovalTimeline = rxMethod<number>(
      pipe(
        tap(() => patchState(store, { irApprovalTimelineLoading: true, irApprovalTimelineError: null })),
        switchMap(irSeq =>
          svc.getIrApprovalTimeline(irSeq).pipe(
            tapResponse({
              next: chain => {
                patchState(store, {
                  irs: store.irs().map(item => (item.irSeq === irSeq ? { ...item, chain } : item)),
                  irApprovalTimelineLoading: false,
                });
              },
              error: (err: unknown) =>
                patchState(store, { irApprovalTimelineLoading: false, irApprovalTimelineError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to load approval timeline') }),
            }),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

    return {
      loadIrs(): void {
        loadIrs();
      },

      loadIrDetail(irSeq: number): void {
        loadIrDetail(irSeq);
      },

      loadIrApprovalTimeline(irSeq: number): void {
        loadIrApprovalTimeline(irSeq);
      },

      loadLinkedRequestWells(): void {
        loadLinkedRequestWells();
      },

      /**
       * No optimistic echo: the backend only ever confirms an `ir_seq` (see WwellIrSaveResult),
       * so this waits out a real follow-up GET (getIrDetailBlocks) and merges *that* onto the
       * record's last known-real state before `irs` ever changes — never onto whatever was just
       * typed. A field with no backing column at all (client, targetAquifer, targetDepthFt... see
       * mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement's own doc comment) simply won't reflect the
       * edit once this settles, since there is nowhere real to read it back from — better an
       * honest revert than a value that only looks saved until the next reload.
       *
       * `creatingIr` stays true for both round-trips (POST, then GET), so the spinner is what
       * covers the extra latency — nothing renders in between.
       */
      createIr(
        payload: CreateIntegratedRequirementPayload,
        irBulkPayload: WwellIrBulkSavePayload,
        attachmentNames: readonly string[] = [],
      ): void {
        patchState(store, { creatingIr: true, createIrError: null });
        svc
          .createIr(irBulkPayload)
          .pipe(
            delay(WWELL_SAVE_DELAY_MS),
            switchMap(irSeq => {
              if (irSeq === null) return of({ irSeq, blocks: null });
              return svc.getIrDetailBlocks(irSeq).pipe(
                map(blocks => ({ irSeq, blocks })),
                // The save itself already succeeded here — a hiccup on this second, read-only
                // call must not read as "the save failed" (see the `blocks === null` branch below).
                catchError(() => of({ irSeq, blocks: null })),
              );
            }),
          )
          .subscribe({
            next: ({ irSeq, blocks }) => {
              if (irSeq === null) {
                // No identity to file this under at all — shouldn't happen for a genuine backend
                // success, so there's nothing to merge or select; just stop spinning.
                patchState(store, { creatingIr: false });
                return;
              }
              if (blocks === null) {
                // Confirmed saved (a real ir_seq came back) but the follow-up detail fetch failed
                // — record the real identity so a resave correctly UPDATEs instead of re-INSERTing,
                // but leave `irs` untouched rather than show anything unconfirmed.
                patchState(store, {
                  draftIrSeq: irSeq,
                  creatingIr: false,
                  createIrError: 'Saved, but the latest data failed to reload — refresh to see it.',
                });
                return;
              }
              const existing = store.irs().find(item => item.irSeq === irSeq);
              const base =
                existing ?? buildIntegratedRequirementFromPayload(payload, { id: `IR-${irSeq}`, code: `IR-${irSeq}`, irSeq }, attachmentNames);
              const merged = mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement(base, blocks);
              patchState(store, {
                irs: upsertByIrSeq(store.irs(), merged),
                draftIrSeq: irSeq,
                creatingIr: false,
              });
            },
            error: (err: unknown) => patchState(store, { creatingIr: false, createIrError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to create integrated requirement') }),
          });
      },

      submitIrForReview(ir: IntegratedRequirement): void {
        patchState(store, { updatingIrStatus: true, updateIrStatusError: null });
        svc
          .submitIrForReview(ir)
          .pipe(delay(WWELL_UPDATE_DELAY_MS))
          .subscribe({
            next: updated => patchState(store, { irs: store.irs().map(item => (item.id === updated.id ? updated : item)), updatingIrStatus: false }),
            error: (err: unknown) =>
              patchState(store, { updatingIrStatus: false, updateIrStatusError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to submit integrated requirement for review') }),
          });
      },

      /** Deletes the whole IR (header row, cascading to casing/tops/hydrogeology/water — see
       *  WwellIrService.deleteIr) and drops it from `irs` on success. `irSeq` must already be a
       *  persisted record's — there's nothing to delete server-side for a local-only draft. */
      deleteIr(irSeq: number): void {
        patchState(store, { deletingIr: true, deleteIrError: null });
        svc
          .deleteIr(irSeq)
          .pipe(delay(WWELL_DELETE_DELAY_MS))
          .subscribe({
            next: () => patchState(store, { irs: store.irs().filter(item => item.irSeq !== irSeq), deletingIr: false }),
            error: (err: unknown) => patchState(store, { deletingIr: false, deleteIrError: errorMessage(err, 'Failed to delete integrated requirement') }),
          });
      },
    };
  }),
);
```

```scss
@use '../../shared/wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;

// Mirrors app-wwell-ir-editor's own `.ire` sizing (wwell-ir-editor.component.scss) so swapping
// shimmer → real content never shifts the surrounding layout.
:host {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  height: 100%;
  min-height: 0;
}

.irel {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  gap: 0.6rem;
  padding: 0.75rem;
}

/* ── Header — mirrors .ire__header ───────────────────────────────────────── */
.irel__header {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  gap: 1rem;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 0.55rem 0.9rem;
}

.irel__heading {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.35rem;
}

.irel__header-actions {
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}

.irel__chip {
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border;

  &--icon {
    width: 28px;
    height: 28px;
    border-radius: 8px;
  }

  &--badge {
    width: 88px;
    height: 22px;
  }
}

/* ── Body: rail + sections, mirrors .ire__body ───────────────────────────── */
.irel__body {
  flex: 1;
  min-height: 0;
  display: flex;
  gap: 0.7rem;
}

.irel__rail {
  flex-shrink: 0;
  width: 196px;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.5rem;
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 0.65rem 0.5rem;
}

.irel__rail-item {
  display: block;
  height: 1.4rem;
  width: 88%;
  border-radius: 6px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, tokens.$border-soft 25%, tokens.$surface-alt 37%, tokens.$border-soft 63%);
  background-size: 400% 100%;
  animation: irel-shimmer 1.4s ease-in-out infinite;

  &--child {
    width: 76%;
    height: 1.15rem;
    margin-left: 0.9rem;
  }
}

.irel__sections {
  flex: 1;
  min-width: 0;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.65rem;
}

/* ── Section card — mirrors .ire__section ────────────────────────────────── */
.irel__card {
  background: tokens.$surface;
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
  border-radius: 12px;
  padding: 0.7rem 0.9rem;
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.5rem;
}

/* ── Read-display grid — mirrors .ire__view-grid / .ire__view-field ─────── */
.irel__grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(140px, 1fr));
  gap: 0.65rem 1.25rem;
}

.irel__field {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.3rem;
}

/* ── Table — mirrors .ire__view-table-wrap / .ire__view-table ───────────── */
.irel__table {
  border: 1px solid tokens.$border;
  border-radius: 8px;
  overflow: hidden;
}

.irel__table-head {
  height: 28px;
  background: tokens.$surface-alt;
  border-bottom: 1px solid tokens.$border;
}

.irel__table-row {
  height: 34px;
  border-bottom: 1px solid tokens.$border-soft;

  &:last-child {
    border-bottom: none;
  }
}

/* ── Shared shimmer bar ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
 * Same gradient/animation as app-wwell-detail-loading's `.wdl__bar` (kept as a separate keyframe
 * name — `irel-shimmer` vs `wdl-shimmer` — since the two components can be in the DOM at once
 * across different pages/tabs and Angular doesn't scope `@keyframes` names). */
.irel__bar {
  display: block;
  height: 0.7rem;
  border-radius: 4px;
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, tokens.$border-soft 25%, tokens.$surface-alt 37%, tokens.$border-soft 63%);
  background-size: 400% 100%;
  animation: irel-shimmer 1.4s ease-in-out infinite;

  &--eyebrow {
    width: 70px;
    height: 0.6rem;
  }

  &--title {
    width: 220px;
    height: 1.1rem;
  }

  &--rail-title {
    width: 60%;
    height: 0.6rem;
    margin-bottom: 0.15rem;
  }

  &--card-title {
    width: 32%;
    height: 0.85rem;
  }

  &--label {
    width: 55%;
    height: 0.55rem;
  }

  &--value {
    width: 75%;
    height: 0.8rem;
  }
}

@keyframes irel-shimmer {
  0% {
    background-position: 100% 50%;
  }

  100% {
    background-position: -100% 50%;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 1100px) {
  .irel__rail {
    width: 160px;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .irel__body {
    flex-direction: column;
  }

  .irel__rail {
    width: 100%;
    flex-direction: row;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
  }

  .irel__rail-item {
    width: 46%;
  }

  .irel__rail-item--child {
    margin-left: 0;
  }
}
```

## [A] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-editor-loading/wwell-ir-editor-loading.component.ts

```ts
import { ChangeDetectionStrategy, Component } from '@angular/core';

/** Pure-decoration shimmer shaped like `app-wwell-ir-editor` itself (header, rail, section cards,
 *  tables) — shown in place of the editor while a selected IR's detail is still loading, so the
 *  swap from shimmer to real content reads as a fill-in rather than a layout jump. Kept as its own
 *  component (rather than reusing the generic `app-wwell-detail-loading`) because that one is
 *  shared by several other master-detail pages (Requests, WLDA/Land Affairs Approvals) whose
 *  detail panel has no rail or tables — matching *this* page's skeleton to the editor's actual
 *  structure would misalign every other consumer. See wwell-ir.component.html's `detailMode()`
 *  branch for which shimmer shows when. */
@Component({
  selector: 'app-wwell-ir-editor-loading',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [],
  templateUrl: './wwell-ir-editor-loading.component.html',
  styleUrl: './wwell-ir-editor-loading.component.scss',
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
})
export class WwellIrEditorLoadingComponent {}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-editor/wwell-ir-detail-rows.util.ts

```ts
import { IntegratedRequirement } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';

/** Flags a value that's actually meaningful, not just informational — e.g. a nonzero H2S
 *  reading should read as amber/danger regardless of which section it sits in. Omitted (or
 *  `'default'`) renders as a plain field. */
export type RowTone = 'default' | 'warning' | 'danger' | 'success';

export interface KvRow {
  readonly label: string;
  readonly value: string;
  readonly tone?: RowTone;
  /** Renders `value` as a small pill instead of plain text — for fields whose value is one of
   *  a fixed set of options (a completion type, a test type, ...), where a badge reads faster
   *  than inline text at a glance. */
  readonly badge?: boolean;
}

export const EMPTY_VALUE = '—';

export function fmtNum(value: number | null | undefined, unit?: string): string {
  if (value === null || value === undefined) return EMPTY_VALUE;
  return unit ? `${value} ${unit}` : `${value}`;
}

export function fmtStr(value: string | null | undefined): string {
  return value ? value : EMPTY_VALUE;
}

/** `linkedRequestId` is the raw prewap_seq (e.g. "5008") — resolve it to the well name shown in
 *  the "Link to Request" picker itself (WwellIrStore.linkedRequestWellOptions) so the view row
 *  reads the same way the dropdown did. Falls back to the raw id if the well has dropped out of
 *  the eligible-wells list (e.g. already linked elsewhere) rather than showing nothing. */
function resolveLinkedRequestLabel(
  linkedRequestId: string | null,
  wellOptions: readonly { readonly value: string; readonly label: string }[],
): string {
  if (!linkedRequestId) return EMPTY_VALUE;
  return wellOptions.find(option => option.value === linkedRequestId)?.label ?? linkedRequestId;
}

/** Mirrors the IR wizard's "Request Reference" step (wwell-ir-form.schema.ts) field-for-field,
 *  so the detail page's sections line up 1:1 with the stepper the data was captured through. */
export function buildRequestReferenceRows(
  ir: IntegratedRequirement,
  linkedRequestWellOptions: readonly { readonly value: string; readonly label: string }[] = [],
): readonly KvRow[] {
  const r = ir.requestReference;
  return [
    { label: 'Link to Request', value: resolveLinkedRequestLabel(r.linkedRequestId, linkedRequestWellOptions) },
    { label: 'Letter Number', value: fmtStr(r.letterNumber) },
    { label: 'BI Number', value: fmtStr(r.client) },
    { label: 'Supporting Business', value: fmtStr(r.supportingBusiness) },
    { label: 'Water Well Name', value: fmtStr(r.wellName) },
    { label: 'Target Aquifer', value: fmtStr(r.targetAquifer), badge: true },
    // A brand-new draft is created through the required-number fallback in
    // mapIrFormValueToSubmission (targetDepthFt ?? 0) before the author has entered a real value —
    // treat a literal 0 as "not yet entered" rather than a misleading real reading (see the
    // matching fallback in mapIntegratedRequirementToFormPatch, wwell-ir-edit.mapper.ts).
    { label: 'Target Depth', value: r.targetDepthFt ? fmtNum(r.targetDepthFt, 'ft') : EMPTY_VALUE },
    { label: 'Surface Elevation', value: `${fmtNum(r.surfaceElevationFtAsl)} ft ASL` },
    { label: 'Coordinates', value: fmtStr(r.coordinates) },
  ];
}

/** Mirrors the schema's "Program Details" section (2.1) — its own rail leaf/edit unit, separate
 *  from Development & Testing (2.2) below. `openHoleSizeIn` only ever means something when the
 *  completion type is 'Open Hole' (see the field's `visibleWhen` gate in the schema), so it's
 *  only included then rather than showing a dangling "—" the rest of the time. */
export function buildProgramMainRows(ir: IntegratedRequirement): readonly KvRow[] {
  const p = ir.programDetails;
  const rows: KvRow[] = [{ label: 'Completion', value: fmtStr(p.completion), badge: true }];

  if (p.completion === 'Open Hole') {
    rows.push({ label: 'Open Hole Size', value: fmtStr(p.openHoleSizeIn) });
  }

  rows.push(
    { label: 'Acidization', value: fmtStr(p.acidization), badge: true },
    { label: 'Transmission', value: fmtStr(p.transmission), badge: true },
  );
  return rows;
}

/** Mirrors the schema's "Development & Testing" section (2.2) — see buildProgramMainRows' doc
 *  comment for why this is a separate function rather than one combined list. */
export function buildProgramDevRows(ir: IntegratedRequirement): readonly KvRow[] {
  const d = ir.programDetails.developmentTesting;
  return [
    { label: 'Logging', value: fmtStr(d.logging), badge: true },
    { label: 'Mud Logging', value: fmtStr(d.mudLogging), badge: true },
    { label: 'Sampling', value: fmtStr(d.sampling), badge: true },
    { label: 'Flow Test', value: fmtStr(d.flowTest), badge: true },
    { label: 'Development/Testing', value: fmtStr(d.remarks) },
  ];
}

/** Mirrors the wizard's "Estimated Hydrology" step, including its test-type-conditional
 *  fields (SIWHP for a flow test; water level + pump depth for a pump test) — see the
 *  matching `visibleWhen` gates on `siwhpPsi`/`estimatedWaterLevelFt`/`estimatedPumpDepthFt`
 *  in wwell-ir-form.schema.ts. Any nonzero H2S gets `tone: 'warning'` — a toxic-gas reading is
 *  worth flagging on sight, not just listed at the same visual weight as TDS/rate. */
export function buildHydrogeologyRows(ir: IntegratedRequirement): readonly KvRow[] {
  const isFlowTest = ir.testProgram.testType === 'flow-test';
  const h2sPpm = ir.hydrogeology.h2sPpm;
  const rows: KvRow[] = [
    { label: 'Aquifer', value: fmtStr(ir.hydrogeology.aquifer), badge: true },
    { label: 'Test Type', value: isFlowTest ? 'Flow Test' : 'Pump Test', badge: true },
    { label: 'Estimated TDS', value: fmtNum(ir.hydrogeology.estimatedTdsPpm, 'ppm') },
    { label: 'Estimated Rate', value: fmtNum(ir.testProgram.estimatedRateGpm, 'GPM') },
    {
      label: 'H2S',
      value: fmtNum(h2sPpm, 'ppm'),
      tone: h2sPpm ? 'warning' : 'default',
    },
  ];

  if (isFlowTest) {
    rows.push({ label: 'SIWHP', value: fmtNum(ir.testProgram.siwhpPsi, 'psi') });
  } else {
    rows.push(
      { label: 'Estimated Water Level', value: fmtNum(ir.testProgram.estimatedWaterLevelFt, 'ft') },
      { label: 'Estimated Pump Depth', value: fmtNum(ir.hydrogeology.estimatedPumpDepthFt, 'ft') },
    );
  }
  return rows;
}

export function buildGeologistContactRows(ir: IntegratedRequirement): readonly KvRow[] {
  const c = ir.contacts;
  return [
    { label: 'Primary', value: fmtStr(c.geologistPrimary) },
    { label: 'Secondary', value: fmtStr(c.geologistSecondary) },
  ];
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-editor/wwell-ir-editor.component.html

```html
<div class="ire">
  <header class="ire__header">
    <div class="ire__heading">
      <p class="ire__eyebrow">{{ ir().code }}</p>
      <h2 class="ire__title">{{ ir().requestReference.wellName || "Untitled Well" }}</h2>
    </div>
    <div class="ire__header-actions">
      <div class="ire__view-toggle" role="group" aria-label="View mode">
        <button
          type="button"
          class="ire__view-toggle-btn"
          [class.ire__view-toggle-btn--active]="viewMode() === 'sections'"
          aria-label="Sections view"
          title="Sections view"
          (click)="setViewMode('sections')"
```
