```ts
import { mapIrFormValueToSubmission } from './wwell-ir-form.mapper';
import { WwellIrFormRawValue } from './wwell-ir-form.types';

function buildRaw(overrides: Partial<WwellIrFormRawValue> = {}): WwellIrFormRawValue {
  return {
    reference: {
      main: {
        linkedRequestId: 'REQ-00198',
        letterNumber: 'LTR-2026-0456',
        client: 'BI-12',
        supportingBusiness: 'Agriculture',
        wellName: 'HAIL-500',
        targetAquifer: 'Saq',
        targetDepthFt: 510,
        surfaceElevationFtAsl: 980,
        coordinates: '27.5114°N, 41.7208°E',
      },
    },
    subsurface: {
      formationTops: [{ name: 'Saq', depthFt: 200, subSeaFtAsl: 780, remarks: 'Top of Saq' }],
      casing: [{ type: 'Conductor', sizeIn: '20"', depthFtBgl: 30, tolFtBgl: null, remarks: 'Cemented' }],
    },
    programDetails: {
      main: { completion: 'Screened', openHoleSizeIn: '', acidization: '', transmission: '' },
      developmentTesting: { logging: 'Caliper', mudLogging: '', sampling: '', flowTest: '', remarks: '' },
    },
    contacts: {
      geologist: { geologistPrimary: 'Eng. A. Al-Mutairi', geologistSecondary: '' },
      offsetWells: [
        {
          epANum: 100,
          distanceKm: 2.5,
          location: 'N 2.5 km',
          aquifer: 'Saq',
          totalDepthFt: 530,
          testDurationMin: 1440,
          rateGpm: 380,
          rpm: 1200,
          swlFt: 85,
          pwlFt: 110,
          drawdownFt: null,
          specificCapacity: null,
          tdsPpm: 1200,
          h2sPpm: 5,
        },
      ],
      remarks: { offsetWellsRemarks: '' },
    },
    hydrogeology: {
      main: {
        testType: 'pump-test',
        aquifer: 'Saq',
        siwhpPsi: null,
        estimatedTdsPpm: 1200,
        estimatedRateGpm: 350,
        h2sPpm: 5,
        estimatedWaterLevelFt: 95,
        estimatedPumpDepthFt: 150,
      },
    },
    attachments: { locationMap: { file: [] }, initiationRequest: { file: [new File(['x'], 'init.pdf')] } },
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe('mapIrFormValueToSubmission', () => {
  it('maps the nested raw form value to the domain payload shape', () => {
    const { payload } = mapIrFormValueToSubmission(buildRaw(), 'in-progress');

    expect(payload.status).toBe('in-progress');
    expect(payload.requestReference.wellName).toBe('HAIL-500');
    expect(payload.requestReference.linkedRequestId).toBe('REQ-00198');
    expect(payload.casing).toEqual([{ type: 'Conductor', sizeIn: '20"', depthFtBgl: 30, tolFtBgl: null, remarks: 'Cemented' }]);
    expect(payload.testProgram.testType).toBe('pump-test');
  });

  it('collects every file-typed field into one flat files array', () => {
    const { files } = mapIrFormValueToSubmission(buildRaw(), 'in-progress');

    expect(files).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(files[0].name).toBe('init.pdf');
  });

  it('normalizes blank optional strings to null', () => {
    const { payload } = mapIrFormValueToSubmission(buildRaw(), 'draft');

    expect(payload.contacts.geologistSecondary).toBeNull();
    expect(payload.offsetWellsRemarks).toBeNull();
    expect(payload.requestReference.linkedRequestId).toBe('REQ-00198');
  });

  it('preserves the requested status, including draft', () => {
    const { payload } = mapIrFormValueToSubmission(buildRaw(), 'draft');
    expect(payload.status).toBe('draft');
  });
});
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-form.mapper.ts

```ts
import {
  CreateIntegratedRequirementPayload,
  IntegratedRequirementStatus,
  IntegratedRequirementTestType,
} from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { WwellIrFormRawValue } from './wwell-ir-form.types';

export interface MappedIrSubmission {
  readonly payload: CreateIntegratedRequirementPayload;
  readonly files: readonly File[];
}

/** Bridges the schema-shaped raw form value to the domain payload — the two are deliberately not the same shape. */
export function mapIrFormValueToSubmission(
  raw: WwellIrFormRawValue,
  status: Extract<IntegratedRequirementStatus, 'draft' | 'in-progress'>,
): MappedIrSubmission {
  const payload: CreateIntegratedRequirementPayload = {
    status,
    requestReference: {
      linkedRequestId: raw.reference.main.linkedRequestId || null,
      letterNumber: raw.reference.main.letterNumber || null,
      client: raw.reference.main.client,
      supportingBusiness: raw.reference.main.supportingBusiness,
      wellName: raw.reference.main.wellName,
      targetAquifer: raw.reference.main.targetAquifer,
      targetDepthFt: raw.reference.main.targetDepthFt ?? 0,
      surfaceElevationFtAsl: raw.reference.main.surfaceElevationFtAsl,
      coordinates: raw.reference.main.coordinates,
    },
    formationTops: raw.subsurface.formationTops.map(row => ({
      name: row.name,
      depthFt: row.depthFt ?? 0,
      subSeaFtAsl: row.subSeaFtAsl,
      remarks: row.remarks || null,
    })),
    casing: raw.subsurface.casing.map(row => ({
      type: row.type,
      sizeIn: row.sizeIn,
      depthFtBgl: row.depthFtBgl ?? 0,
      tolFtBgl: row.tolFtBgl,
      remarks: row.remarks || null,
    })),
    programDetails: {
      completion: raw.programDetails.main.completion,
      openHoleSizeIn: raw.programDetails.main.openHoleSizeIn || null,
      acidization: raw.programDetails.main.acidization,
      transmission: raw.programDetails.main.transmission,
      developmentTesting: { ...raw.programDetails.developmentTesting },
    },
    contacts: {
      geologistPrimary: raw.contacts.geologist.geologistPrimary,
      geologistSecondary: raw.contacts.geologist.geologistSecondary || null,
    },
    offsetWells: raw.contacts.offsetWells.map(row => ({
      epANum: row.epANum ?? 0,
      distanceKm: row.distanceKm,
      location: row.location || null,
      aquifer: row.aquifer || null,
      totalDepthFt: row.totalDepthFt,
      testDurationMin: row.testDurationMin,
      rateGpm: row.rateGpm,
      rpm: row.rpm,
      swlFt: row.swlFt,
      pwlFt: row.pwlFt,
      drawdownFt: row.drawdownFt,
      specificCapacity: row.specificCapacity,
      tdsPpm: row.tdsPpm,
      h2sPpm: row.h2sPpm,
    })),
    offsetWellsRemarks: raw.contacts.remarks.offsetWellsRemarks || null,
    hydrogeology: {
      aquifer: raw.hydrogeology.main.aquifer,
      estimatedTdsPpm: raw.hydrogeology.main.estimatedTdsPpm,
      h2sPpm: raw.hydrogeology.main.h2sPpm,
      estimatedPumpDepthFt: raw.hydrogeology.main.estimatedPumpDepthFt,
    },
    testProgram: {
      testType: raw.hydrogeology.main.testType as IntegratedRequirementTestType,
      estimatedRateGpm: raw.hydrogeology.main.estimatedRateGpm,
      estimatedWaterLevelFt: raw.hydrogeology.main.estimatedWaterLevelFt,
      siwhpPsi: raw.hydrogeology.main.siwhpPsi,
    },
  };

  const files = [...raw.attachments.locationMap.file, ...raw.attachments.initiationRequest.file];

  return { payload, files };
}
```

```ts
import type { Signal } from '@angular/core';
import { WwellFieldOption, WwellFormStepConfig } from '../../shared/dynamic-form/wwell-dynamic-form.types';
import {
  WWELL_APPROVAL_COORDINATES_PATTERN,
  WWELL_APPROVAL_WELL_NAME_PATTERN,
} from '../../shared/wwell-approvals/wwell-approvals.config';
import {
  WWELL_ACIDIZATION_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_CASING_TYPE_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_CLIENT_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_COMPLETION_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_DEVELOPMENT_FLOW_TEST_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_DEVELOPMENT_TESTING_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_GEOLOGIST_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_LOGGING_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_MUD_LOGGING_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_SAMPLING_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_SUPPORTING_BUSINESS_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_TEST_TYPE_OPTIONS,
  WWELL_TRANSMISSION_OPTIONS,
} from '../../shared/wwell-planning-reference-data.config';
import { WWELL_IR_FORMATION_OPTIONS } from './wwell-ir-form.config';

/** Single source of truth for the IR stepper — add a field here, not in a template. */
export const WWELL_IR_FORM_SCHEMA: readonly WwellFormStepConfig[] = [
  {
    key: 'reference',
    label: 'Request Reference',
    sections: [
      {
        key: 'main',
        title: null,
        fields: [
          // Static [] placeholder — buildWwellIrFormSchema() below swaps this for a live Signal
          // (backed by GET /planning/api/v1/wells/eligible-for-ir) before this schema is rendered.
          // Required: it maps to exad_gwd_ir_header.prewap_seq, which is NOT NULL in production
          // (docs/ir/EXAD_GWD_IR_HEADER.md) — without this the save fails at the database with
          // ORA-01400 instead of telling the user which field is missing.
          {
            key: 'linkedRequestId',
            label: 'Link to Request',
            type: 'select',
            options: [],
            validators: { required: true },
            searchable: true,
          },
          { key: 'letterNumber', label: 'Letter Number', type: 'text', placeholder: 'e.g., LTR-2026-0456' },
          { key: 'client', label: 'BI Number', type: 'select', options: WWELL_CLIENT_OPTIONS, validators: { required: true } },
          {
            key: 'supportingBusiness',
            label: 'Supporting Business',
            type: 'select',
            options: WWELL_SUPPORTING_BUSINESS_OPTIONS,
            validators: { required: true },
          },
          {
            key: 'wellName',
            label: 'Water Well Name',
            type: 'text',
            placeholder: 'e.g., HAIL-500',
            validators: { required: true, pattern: WWELL_APPROVAL_WELL_NAME_PATTERN },
          },
          {
            key: 'targetAquifer',
            label: 'Target Aquifer',
            type: 'select',
            options: WWELL_IR_FORMATION_OPTIONS,
            validators: { required: true },
          },
          {
            key: 'targetDepthFt',
            label: 'Target Depth',
            type: 'number',
            suffix: 'ft',
            validators: { required: true, min: 0 },
          },
          {
            key: 'surfaceElevationFtAsl',
            label: 'Surface Elevation',
            type: 'number',
            suffix: 'ft ASL',
            hint: 'Used for auto-calculating sub-sea depths',
          },
          {
            // Odd field out in this section's otherwise-even count — colSpan:2 keeps it from
            // landing alone in a row with a dangling empty cell beside it (also just genuinely
            // useful room for a lat/long string).
            key: 'coordinates',
            label: 'Coordinates',
            type: 'text',
            placeholder: 'e.g., 27.5114°N, 41.7208°E',
            validators: { required: true, pattern: WWELL_APPROVAL_COORDINATES_PATTERN },
            colSpan: 2,
          },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    key: 'subsurface',
    label: 'Subsurface & Construction',
    sections: [
      {
        key: 'formationTops',
        title: 'Formation Tops',
        repeatable: {
          key: 'formationTops',
          addLabel: '+ Add Formation Top',
          minRequiredRows: 1,
          rowFields: [
            { key: 'name', label: 'Formation', type: 'select', options: WWELL_IR_FORMATION_OPTIONS },
            { key: 'depthFt', label: 'Depth', type: 'number', suffix: 'ft' },
            { key: 'subSeaFtAsl', label: 'Sub-sea', type: 'number', suffix: 'ft ASL' },
            {
              // colSpan:2 (== full row width in a repeatable row's variable-column grid, see
              // `.dyf--span-2`'s doc comment) — the 4th field in a row that otherwise fits 3
              // columns would land alone in a cramped 1-column-wide row without this.
              key: 'remarks',
              label: 'Remarks',
              type: 'text',
              placeholder: 'e.g., Top of Umm Er Radhuma',
              colSpan: 2,
            },
          ],
        },
      },
      {
        key: 'casing',
        title: 'Casing',
        repeatable: {
          key: 'casing',
          addLabel: '+ Add Casing',
          minRequiredRows: 1,
          rowFields: [
            {
              key: 'type',
              label: 'Type',
              type: 'select',
              options: WWELL_CASING_TYPE_OPTIONS,
              placeholder: 'e.g., Conductor',
            },
            { key: 'sizeIn', label: 'Size', type: 'text', placeholder: 'e.g., 20"' },
            { key: 'depthFtBgl', label: 'Depth', type: 'number', suffix: 'ft bgl' },
            {
              key: 'tolFtBgl',
              label: 'Top Of Liner (TOL)',
              type: 'number',
              suffix: 'ft bgl',
              hint: 'Depth this liner hangs from',
              visibleWhen: { field: 'type', equals: 'Liner' },
            },
            {
              // colSpan:2 — same "don't orphan the trailing free-text field" fix as
              // Formation Tops' Remarks above.
              key: 'remarks',
              label: 'Remarks',
              type: 'text',
              placeholder: 'e.g., Cemented',
              colSpan: 2,
            },
          ],
        },
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    key: 'programDetails',
    label: 'Program Details',
    sections: [
      {
        // One combined section — Program Details (completion/acidization/transmission) and
        // Development & Testing (logging/mudLogging/sampling/flowTest/remarks) used to be two
        // separately edited/saved sections; merged into one so they read as a single block
        // (matching the reference GWD/AGWA screen) instead of two stacked cards.
        //
        // Every field here is full-width (`colSpan: 2`), stacked one per row — a 2-column grid
        // made sense for the old single-line combo inputs, but these are big resizable textareas
        // now (see WwellRichTextareaComponent) that can legitimately hold several lines each;
        // sitting two per row would either cramp them or waste half the row's height. Plain
        // top-to-bottom order, not interleaved — there's no column pairing left to preserve.
        key: 'main',
        title: null,
        fields: [
          // Same control as its siblings below (dropdown-checkbox + big textarea) rather than a
          // strict `select`, per explicit request for visual/behavioral consistency across the
          // whole section. `openHoleSizeIn`'s `visibleWhen` and the wellbore diagram's completion
          // switch (wwell-ir-wellbore-diagram.mapper.ts) both still match on an EXACT string equal
          // to one of WWELL_COMPLETION_OPTIONS — that holds as long as exactly one option is
          // checked (the expected case), but checking more than one now produces a multi-line
          // value that matches none of them, same trade-off every other field here already accepts.
          {
            key: 'completion',
            label: 'Completion',
            type: 'rich-textarea',
            options: WWELL_COMPLETION_OPTIONS,
            validators: { required: true },
            colSpan: 2,
          },
          {
            key: 'openHoleSizeIn',
            label: 'Open Hole Size',
            type: 'text',
            placeholder: 'e.g., 8 1/2"',
            hint: 'Bit size below the last casing shoe',
            visibleWhen: { field: 'completion', equals: 'Open Hole' },
            colSpan: 2,
          },
          { key: 'acidization', label: 'Acidization', type: 'rich-textarea', options: WWELL_ACIDIZATION_OPTIONS, colSpan: 2 },
          { key: 'transmission', label: 'Transmission', type: 'rich-textarea', options: WWELL_TRANSMISSION_OPTIONS, colSpan: 2 },
          { key: 'logging', label: 'Logging', type: 'rich-textarea', options: WWELL_LOGGING_OPTIONS, colSpan: 2 },
          { key: 'mudLogging', label: 'Mud Logging', type: 'rich-textarea', options: WWELL_MUD_LOGGING_OPTIONS, colSpan: 2 },

            options: WWELL_IR_FORMATION_OPTIONS,
            validators: { required: true },
          },
          { key: 'estimatedTdsPpm', label: 'Estimated TDS', type: 'number', suffix: 'ppm' },
          { key: 'estimatedRateGpm', label: 'Estimated Rate', type: 'number', suffix: 'GPM' },
          { key: 'h2sPpm', label: 'H2S', type: 'number', suffix: 'ppm' },
          // Mode-specific fields are grouped last (after an even count of always-visible fields above)
          // so a collapsed field never shares a grid row with an unrelated visible one.
          {
            key: 'siwhpPsi',
            label: 'SIWHP',
            type: 'number',
            suffix: 'psi',
            colSpan: 2,
            visibleWhen: { field: 'testType', equals: 'flow-test' },
          },
          {
            key: 'estimatedWaterLevelFt',
            label: 'Estimated Water Level',
            type: 'number',
            suffix: 'ft',
            visibleWhen: { field: 'testType', equals: 'pump-test' },

          {
            key: 'estimatedPumpDepthFt',
            label: 'Estimated Pump Depth',
            type: 'number',
            suffix: 'ft',
            visibleWhen: { field: 'testType', equals: 'pump-test' },

      },

  },
  {
    // Split into two single-field sections (rather than one 'files' section holding both) so each
    // becomes its own scrollable IR SECTIONS nav target (7.0 Location Map / 8.0 Initiation
    // Request) — see wwell-ir-section-rail.config.ts.
    key: 'attachments',
    label: 'Attachments & Review',
    sections: [
      {
        key: 'locationMap',
        title: 'Location Map',
        fields: [{ key: 'file', label: 'Location Map', type: 'file', placeholder: 'Click to attach file...' }],
      },
      {
        key: 'initiationRequest',
        title: 'Initiation Request',
        fields: [
          {
            key: 'file',
            label: 'Initiation Request',
            type: 'file',
            placeholder: 'Click to attach file...',
            validators: { required: true },
          },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
];

/**
 * Returns a copy of WWELL_IR_FORM_SCHEMA with the 'reference'/'main' section's 'linkedRequestId'
 * field wired to `linkedRequestOptions` instead of its static `[]` placeholder. The schema array
 * itself is a module-level constant (no injector available at definition time), so a store-backed
 * Signal can only be attached here, once, by the component that actually has one — see
 * WwellIrEditorComponent's `schema` field.
 */
export function buildWwellIrFormSchema(
  linkedRequestOptions: Signal<readonly WwellFieldOption[]>,
): readonly WwellFormStepConfig[] {
  return WWELL_IR_FORM_SCHEMA.map(step => {
    if (step.key !== 'reference') return step;
    return {
      ...step,
      sections: step.sections.map(section => {
        if (section.key !== 'main' || !section.fields) return section;
        return {
          ...section,
          fields: section.fields.map(field =>
            field.key === 'linkedRequestId' ? { ...field, options: linkedRequestOptions } : field,
          ),
        };
      }),
    };
  });
}
```

```ts
/** Mirrors WWELL_IR_FORM_SCHEMA's nested FormGroup shape 1:1 — hand-written since the schema is runtime data. */

export interface WwellIrRawReference {
  readonly linkedRequestId: string;
  readonly letterNumber: string;
  readonly client: string;
  readonly supportingBusiness: string;
  readonly wellName: string;
  readonly targetAquifer: string;
  readonly targetDepthFt: number | null;
  readonly surfaceElevationFtAsl: number | null;
  readonly coordinates: string;
}

export interface WwellIrRawFormationTop {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly depthFt: number | null;
  readonly subSeaFtAsl: number | null;
  readonly remarks: string;
}

export interface WwellIrRawCasingRow {
  readonly type: string;
  readonly sizeIn: string;
  readonly depthFtBgl: number | null;
  /** Only meaningful when `type === 'Liner'` — see the `visibleWhen` gate on this field in wwell-ir-form.schema.ts. */
  readonly tolFtBgl: number | null;
  readonly remarks: string;
}

export interface WwellIrRawProgramMain {
  readonly completion: string;
  readonly openHoleSizeIn: string;
  readonly acidization: string;
  readonly transmission: string;
}

export interface WwellIrRawDevelopmentTesting {
  readonly logging: string;
  readonly mudLogging: string;
  readonly sampling: string;
  readonly flowTest: string;
  readonly remarks: string;
}

/** Flat shape of the IR editor's merged "Program Details" FormGroup — Program Details
 *  (completion/acidization/transmission) and Development & Testing (logging/mudLogging/sampling/
 *  flowTest/remarks) are one combined, one-pencil-edit section there (see
 *  wwell-ir-editor.component.ts's `programEdit`) even though they still split back into `main`/
 *  `developmentTesting` on the way to `WwellIrFormRawValue`. */
export type WwellIrRawProgramDetails = WwellIrRawProgramMain & WwellIrRawDevelopmentTesting;

export interface WwellIrRawGeologist {
  readonly geologistPrimary: string;
  readonly geologistSecondary: string;
}

export interface WwellIrRawOffsetWell {
  readonly epANum: number | null;
  readonly distanceKm: number | null;
  readonly location: string;
  readonly aquifer: string;
  readonly totalDepthFt: number | null;
  readonly testDurationMin: number | null;
  readonly rateGpm: number | null;
  readonly rpm: number | null;
  readonly swlFt: number | null;
  readonly pwlFt: number | null;
  /** Disabled/computed control — see the `readonly` flag on this field in wwell-ir-form.schema.ts. */
  readonly drawdownFt: number | null;
  readonly specificCapacity: number | null;
  readonly tdsPpm: number | null;
  readonly h2sPpm: number | null;
}

}

  readonly estimatedPumpDepthFt: number | null;
}

}

  };
}
```

## [A] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-legacy-html.util.ts

```ts
/**
 * Several `exad_gwd_ir_header` free-text remarks columns — `completion`, `acidization`, `logging`,
 * `mud_rmks`, `sampling`, `flow_rmks` — carry raw markup on older records instead of plain text:
 * a "Save As HTML" dump straight out of a legacy Windows RTF/rich-text control, e.g.
 * `<HTML><BODY><P ALIGN="left"><FONT FACE="Arial" SIZE="11" COLOR="#000000">7" diameter, 0.012"
 * slots</FONT></P></BODY></HTML>`, sometimes several such `<HTML>...</HTML>` documents
 * concatenated back-to-back in one column value (each `<P>` was originally its own remark line).
 * Left as-is, every place that shows one of these fields — the KV read cards
 * (wwell-ir-detail-rows.util.ts), the editor's combo/select inputs, the printable Letter view —
 * renders the raw tags instead of the remark text.
 *
 * `mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement` (wwell-ir-detail-response.mapper.ts) is the one
 * place backend column values become `IntegratedRequirement` field values, for both the initial
 * detail fetch on selecting a record and the post-save refetch — running every legacy-prone field
 * through `stripLegacyIrHtml` there means every downstream reader (including the editor's own
 * raw-form mapper, which a re-save resends unchanged for any section the user didn't open) only
 * ever sees plain text, so a save after that point persists plain text back to the backend even
 * though the column itself started out as markup.
 */
const LEGACY_HTML_DOCUMENT_PATTERN = /<html[\s>]/i;

/** True when `value` is one of these legacy `<HTML>...` dumps rather than plain text. A brand-new
 *  record's plain, user-typed remarks (or a blank value) always fail this check. */
export function isLegacyIrHtmlText(value: string | null | undefined): boolean {
  return !!value && LEGACY_HTML_DOCUMENT_PATTERN.test(value);
}

/** Collapses runs of horizontal whitespace within each line (left by adjacent `<FONT>` run
 *  boundaries) while keeping real paragraph breaks (`\n`, inserted for `<P>`/`<BR>`/separate
 *  `<HTML>` documents below) intact, and drops any resulting blank line. */
function collapseWhitespacePreservingLines(text: string): string {
  return text
    .split('\n')
    .map(line => line.replace(/[^\S\n]+/g, ' ').trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join('\n');
}

/**
 * Strips a legacy `exad_gwd_ir_header` rich-text remarks value down to the plain text it actually
 * displays. Values that AREN'T legacy markup (a plain string, blank, or already-cleaned text from
 * a prior parse) pass through unchanged, so this is safe to call unconditionally on every
 * legacy-prone field rather than only on ones already known to carry HTML.
 *
 * Uses `DOMParser` rather than a hand-rolled tag-stripping regex so that whitespace between
 * adjacent `<FONT>` runs is preserved (or not) exactly as a browser would actually render that
 * markup — the legacy source sometimes omits the space between two runs, and the "exact
 * information" is whatever text the field actually displayed, gaps included.
 */
export function stripLegacyIrHtml(value: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!value) return '';
  if (!isLegacyIrHtmlText(value)) return value.trim();

  const parser = new DOMParser();
  const paragraphs = value
    // Multiple concatenated `<HTML>...</HTML>` documents in one column value are separate
    // remark lines saved back-to-back — split them apart before parsing, since DOMParser only
    // ever reads the first document out of a string containing several.
    .split(/(?=<html[\s>])/i)
    .map(document => document.replace(/<br\s*\/?>/gi, '\n').replace(/<\/p>/gi, '\n'))
    .map(document => parser.parseFromString(document, 'text/html').body.textContent ?? '')
    .map(collapseWhitespacePreservingLines)
    .filter(Boolean);

  return paragraphs.join('\n');
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-payload.mapper.spec.ts

```ts
import {
  IntegratedRequirementCasingRow,
  IntegratedRequirementFormationTop,
  IntegratedRequirementHydrogeology,
  IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell,
  IntegratedRequirementTestProgram,
} from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload, WwellIrSaveContext } from './wwell-ir-payload.mapper';
import { WwellIrFormRawValue } from './wwell-ir-form.types';
import { IrCasingRow, IrTopsRow, IrWaterRow } from './wwell-ir-payload.model';

function buildRaw(overrides: Partial<WwellIrFormRawValue> = {}): WwellIrFormRawValue {
  return {
    reference: {
      main: {
        linkedRequestId: 'REQ-00198',
        letterNumber: 'LTR-2026-0456',
        client: 'BI-12',
        supportingBusiness: 'Agriculture',
        wellName: 'HAIL-500',
        targetAquifer: 'Saq',
        targetDepthFt: 510,
        surfaceElevationFtAsl: 980,
        coordinates: '27.5114°N, 41.7208°E',
      },
    },
    subsurface: {
      formationTops: [{ name: 'ARUM', depthFt: 1855, subSeaFtAsl: 1015, remarks: '' }],
      casing: [{ type: 'Conductor', sizeIn: '24"', depthFtBgl: 100, tolFtBgl: null, remarks: 'Cemented' }],
    },
    programDetails: {
      main: { completion: 'Screened', openHoleSizeIn: '', acidization: '', transmission: '' },
      developmentTesting: { logging: '', mudLogging: '', sampling: '', flowTest: '', remarks: '' },
    },
    contacts: {
      geologist: { geologistPrimary: 'Eng. A. Al-Mutairi', geologistSecondary: '' },
      offsetWells: [],
      remarks: { offsetWellsRemarks: '' },
    },
    hydrogeology: {
      main: {
        testType: 'pump-test',
        aquifer: 'WASIA',
        siwhpPsi: null,
        estimatedTdsPpm: 3000,
        estimatedRateGpm: 500,
        h2sPpm: null,
        estimatedWaterLevelFt: 72,
        estimatedPumpDepthFt: null,
      },
    },
    attachments: { locationMap: { file: [] }, initiationRequest: { file: [] } },
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe('buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload', () => {
  it('tags every row INSERT and leaves ir_seq null for a brand-new IR', () => {
    const [header, casing, hydrogeology, tops] = buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload(buildRaw());

    expect(header.data?.[0].action).toBe('INSERT');
    expect(header.data?.[0].ir_seq).toBeNull();
    expect(casing.data?.[0].action).toBe('INSERT');
    expect(hydrogeology.data?.[0].action).toBe('INSERT');
    expect(tops.data?.[0].action).toBe('INSERT');
  });

  it('tags every row UPDATE and threads the known ir_seq once one exists', () => {
    const [header, casing] = buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload(buildRaw(), { irSeq: 8992 });

    expect(header.data?.[0].action).toBe('UPDATE');
    expect(header.data?.[0].ir_seq).toBe(8992);
    expect(casing.data?.[0].ir_seq).toBe(8992);
  });

  it('maps casing and formation-top rows to their exad_gwd_ir_* column names', () => {
    const [, casing, , tops] = buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload(buildRaw());

    expect(casing.data?.[0]).toMatchObject({ csg_size: '24"', csg_type: 'Conductor', csg_depth: 100, csg_remarks: 'Cemented' });
    expect(tops.data?.[0]).toMatchObject({ st_long_cd: 'ARUM', plan_tvd_dpth: 1855, plan_ss_dpth: 1015 });
  });

  it('omits the hydrogeology row when the required aquifer field is still blank (mid-draft)', () => {
    const [, , hydrogeology] = buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload(
      buildRaw({ hydrogeology: { main: { ...buildRaw().hydrogeology.main, aquifer: '' } } }),
    );

    expect(hydrogeology.data).toBeNull();
  });

  it('drops fully-blank repeatable rows so an empty draft does not send junk INSERTs', () => {
    const blankRaw = buildRaw({
      subsurface: {
        formationTops: [{ name: '', depthFt: null, subSeaFtAsl: null, remarks: '' }],
        casing: [{ type: '', sizeIn: '', depthFtBgl: null, tolFtBgl: null, remarks: '' }],
      },
    });

    const [, casing, , tops] = buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload(blankRaw);

    expect(casing.data).toBeNull();
    expect(tops.data).toBeNull();
  });

  it('always emits the water table block with null data — not yet captured by this form', () => {
    const [, , , , water] = buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload(buildRaw());

    expect(water.table).toBe('exad_gwd_ir_water');
    expect(water.data).toBeNull();
  });
});

/** Every repeatable section's `existing`-diff branch (see buildCasingRows/buildTopsRows/
 *  buildWaterRows in wwell-ir-payload.mapper.ts) needs a full `existing` record, even when a
 *  given test only cares about one section — the other four fall back to inert empty/blank
 *  values so they never contribute rows of their own to the assertions below. */
function buildExistingBase(
  overrides: Partial<{
    casing: readonly IntegratedRequirementCasingRow[];
    formationTops: readonly IntegratedRequirementFormationTop[];
    offsetWells: readonly IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell[];
    hydrogeology: IntegratedRequirementHydrogeology;
    testProgram: IntegratedRequirementTestProgram;
  }> = {},
): NonNullable<WwellIrSaveContext['existing']> {
  return {
    casing: [],
    formationTops: [],
    offsetWells: [],
    hydrogeology: { aquifer: '', estimatedTdsPpm: null, h2sPpm: null, estimatedPumpDepthFt: null },
    testProgram: { testType: 'pump-test', estimatedRateGpm: null, estimatedWaterLevelFt: null },
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe('buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload — existing-record diff (update/insert/delete combinations)', () => {
  it('casing: keeps an unchanged row as a no-op, tags a brand-new row INSERT, and tags a removed row DELETE — all in one save', () => {
    const existing = buildExistingBase({
      casing: [
        { type: 'Conductor', sizeIn: '24"', depthFtBgl: 100, tolFtBgl: null, remarks: 'Cemented' },
        { type: 'Surface', sizeIn: '13 3/8"', depthFtBgl: 500, tolFtBgl: null, remarks: null },
      ],
    });

    const raw = buildRaw({
      subsurface: {
        formationTops: [],
        casing: [
          // Same natural key (type + size) and same writable fields as the existing Conductor
          // row — the user left this one alone this save.
          { type: 'Conductor', sizeIn: '24"', depthFtBgl: 100, tolFtBgl: null, remarks: 'Cemented' },
          // No matching existing row — the user added this one.
          { type: 'Production', sizeIn: '9 5/8"', depthFtBgl: 900, tolFtBgl: null, remarks: 'New run' },
          // The Surface row from `existing` is simply absent — the user removed it in the UI.
        ],
      },
    });

    const [, casing] = buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload(raw, { irSeq: 7001, existing });
    const rows = casing.data as IrCasingRow[];

    expect(rows).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(rows.find(row => row.csg_type === 'Conductor')).toMatchObject({ action: '', ir_seq: 7001 });
    expect(rows.find(row => row.csg_type === 'Production')).toMatchObject({ action: 'INSERT', ir_seq: null });
    expect(rows.find(row => row.csg_type === 'Surface')).toMatchObject({ action: 'DELETE', ir_seq: 7001 });
  });

  it('casing: tags a kept row UPDATE once one of its writable fields actually changed', () => {
    const existing = buildExistingBase({
      casing: [{ type: 'Conductor', sizeIn: '24"', depthFtBgl: 100, tolFtBgl: null, remarks: 'Cemented' }],
    });
    const raw = buildRaw({
      subsurface: {
        formationTops: [],
        casing: [{ type: 'Conductor', sizeIn: '24"', depthFtBgl: 120, tolFtBgl: null, remarks: 'Cemented' }],
      },
    });

    const [, casing] = buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload(raw, { irSeq: 7001, existing });
    const rows = casing.data as IrCasingRow[];

    expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(rows[0]).toMatchObject({ action: 'UPDATE', ir_seq: 7001, csg_depth: 120 });
  });
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-payload.mapper.ts

```ts
import {
  IntegratedRequirement,
  IntegratedRequirementCasingRow,
  IntegratedRequirementFormationTop,
  IntegratedRequirementHydrogeology,
  IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell,
  IntegratedRequirementTestProgram,
} from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import {
  WwellIrFormRawValue,
  WwellIrRawCasingRow,
  WwellIrRawFormationTop,
  WwellIrRawHydrology,
  WwellIrRawOffsetWell,
} from './wwell-ir-form.types';
import {
  IrCasingRow,
  IrHeaderRow,
  IrHydrogeologyRow,
  IrRowAction,
  IrTopsRow,
  IrWaterRow,
  WwellIrBulkSavePayload,
} from './wwell-ir-payload.model';
import {
  WWELL_GEOLOGIST_ID_BY_NAME,
  WWELL_TRANSMISSION_CODE_BY_LABEL,
} from '../../shared/wwell-planning-reference-data.config';

/**
 * Carries the previously-assigned ir_seq (if any) so a repeat save UPDATEs instead of
 * re-INSERTing, plus (when known) the record as it was last loaded/saved — used to diff each
 * repeatable row's OWN action instead of inheriting one blanket action for the whole payload.
 * `existing` is intentionally distinguished from "omitted": omitting it (or passing `null`) falls
 * back to the old whole-payload action (every row shares `resolveRowAction(irSeq)`) — the only
 * safe choice when the caller has no snapshot to diff against, since guessing would otherwise
 * risk re-labeling an already-persisted row as a fresh INSERT (duplicate) or a still-current row
 * as DELETE (data loss).
 */
export interface WwellIrSaveContext {
  readonly irSeq: number | null;
  readonly existing?: Pick<
    IntegratedRequirement,
    'casing' | 'formationTops' | 'hydrogeology' | 'testProgram' | 'offsetWells'
  > | null;
}

const NEW_IR_CONTEXT: WwellIrSaveContext = { irSeq: null, existing: null };

function resolveRowAction(irSeq: number | null): IrRowAction {
  return irSeq === null ? 'INSERT' : 'UPDATE';
}

const isBlankCasingRow = (row: WwellIrRawCasingRow): boolean =>
  !row.type && !row.sizeIn && !row.remarks && row.depthFtBgl == null;

const isBlankFormationTop = (row: WwellIrRawFormationTop): boolean =>
  !row.name && !row.remarks && row.depthFt == null && row.subSeaFtAsl == null;

/** Natural keys the backend's UPDATE/DELETE branches match repeatable rows by (see
 *  EXAD_AGWA_IUD_PKG.save_casing/save_tops in 05-agwa-iud-package.sql) — a row whose key isn't
 *  already present in `existing` is genuinely new and must be sent as INSERT, not UPDATE, or the
 *  backend's `WHERE <key match>` finds nothing and silently drops it. */
const casingKey = (row: { readonly type: string; readonly sizeIn: string }): string => `${row.type} ${row.sizeIn}`;
const topKey = (row: { readonly name: string }): string => row.name;

/** Only the columns save_casing's/save_tops' UPDATE branch actually writes (type/size are the
 *  match key itself, not a writable column) — comparing anything else could flag a row "changed"
 *  when nothing the backend would touch actually differs. */
const casingRowUnchanged = (existingRow: IntegratedRequirementCasingRow, row: WwellIrRawCasingRow): boolean =>
  existingRow.depthFtBgl === (row.depthFtBgl ?? 0) && (existingRow.remarks ?? null) === (row.remarks || null);

const topRowUnchanged = (existingRow: IntegratedRequirementFormationTop, row: WwellIrRawFormationTop): boolean =>
  existingRow.depthFt === (row.depthFt ?? 0) && (existingRow.subSeaFtAsl ?? null) === (row.subSeaFtAsl ?? null);

function buildCasingRows(
  raw: readonly WwellIrRawCasingRow[],
  irSeq: number | null,
  existing: readonly IntegratedRequirementCasingRow[] | null | undefined,
): IrCasingRow[] {
  const currentRows = raw.filter(row => !isBlankCasingRow(row));

  if (existing == null) {
    const action = resolveRowAction(irSeq);
    return currentRows.map(row => ({
      action,
      ir_seq: irSeq,
      csg_size: row.sizeIn,
      csg_type: row.type,
      csg_depth: row.depthFtBgl ?? 0,
      csg_remarks: row.remarks || null,
    }));
  }

  const existingByKey = new Map(existing.map(row => [casingKey(row), row]));
  const keptKeys = new Set<string>();

  // A row this save didn't actually change is sent as '' (no-op), not a redundant UPDATE — see
  // IrRowAction's doc comment: the backend's per-row action switch then skips the DML entirely
  // instead of re-writing a row with the exact values it already has on every single save.
  const upserts: IrCasingRow[] = currentRows.map(row => {
    const key = casingKey(row);
    const existingRow = existingByKey.get(key);
    if (existingRow) keptKeys.add(key);
    const action: IrRowAction = existingRow ? (casingRowUnchanged(existingRow, row) ? '' : 'UPDATE') : 'INSERT';
    return {
      action,
      ir_seq: existingRow ? irSeq : null,
      csg_size: row.sizeIn,
      csg_type: row.type,
      csg_depth: row.depthFtBgl ?? 0,
      csg_remarks: row.remarks || null,
    };
  });

  const deletes: IrCasingRow[] = existing
    .filter(row => !keptKeys.has(casingKey(row)))
    .map(row => ({
      action: 'DELETE',
      ir_seq: irSeq,
      csg_size: row.sizeIn,
      csg_type: row.type,
      csg_depth: row.depthFtBgl,
      csg_remarks: row.remarks,
    }));

  return [...upserts, ...deletes];
}

function buildTopsRows(
  raw: readonly WwellIrRawFormationTop[],
  irSeq: number | null,
  existing: readonly IntegratedRequirementFormationTop[] | null | undefined,
): IrTopsRow[] {
  const currentRows = raw.filter(row => !isBlankFormationTop(row));

  if (existing == null) {
    const action = resolveRowAction(irSeq);
    return currentRows.map(row => ({
      action,
      ir_seq: irSeq,
      st_long_cd: row.name,
      plan_tvd_dpth: row.depthFt ?? 0,
      plan_ss_dpth: row.subSeaFtAsl,
    }));
  }

  const existingByKey = new Map(existing.map(row => [topKey(row), row]));
  const keptKeys = new Set<string>();

  const upserts: IrTopsRow[] = currentRows.map(row => {
    const key = topKey(row);
    const existingRow = existingByKey.get(key);
    if (existingRow) keptKeys.add(key);
    const action: IrRowAction = existingRow ? (topRowUnchanged(existingRow, row) ? '' : 'UPDATE') : 'INSERT';
    return {
      action,
      ir_seq: existingRow ? irSeq : null,
      st_long_cd: row.name,
      plan_tvd_dpth: row.depthFt ?? 0,
      plan_ss_dpth: row.subSeaFtAsl,
    };
  });

  const deletes: IrTopsRow[] = existing
    .filter(row => !keptKeys.has(topKey(row)))
    .map(row => ({
      action: 'DELETE',
      ir_seq: irSeq,
      st_long_cd: row.name,
      plan_tvd_dpth: row.depthFt,
      plan_ss_dpth: row.subSeaFtAsl ?? null,
    }));

  return [...upserts, ...deletes];
}

/** True when nothing save_hydrogeology's UPDATE branch would actually write has changed —
 *  est_sta_water_lvl/est_productivity come from the form's (and domain model's) testProgram
 *  section, not hydrogeology, so both are needed to tell "unchanged" from "changed". */
const hydrogeologyRowUnchanged = (
  existingHydro: IntegratedRequirementHydrogeology,
  existingTestProgram: IntegratedRequirementTestProgram,
  raw: WwellIrRawHydrology,
): boolean =>
  existingTestProgram.estimatedWaterLevelFt === raw.estimatedWaterLevelFt &&
  existingHydro.estimatedTdsPpm === raw.estimatedTdsPpm &&
  existingTestProgram.estimatedRateGpm === raw.estimatedRateGpm &&
  existingHydro.h2sPpm === raw.h2sPpm;

/** Only ever 0 or 1 row (the form has a single hydrogeology section, not a repeatable list), keyed
 *  by aquifer (st_long_cd) the same way the backend matches it — so switching to a different
 *  aquifer must DELETE the old row, not silently orphan it under a key nothing will ever match again. */
function buildHydrogeologyRows(
  raw: WwellIrRawHydrology,
  irSeq: number | null,
  existing: Pick<IntegratedRequirement, 'hydrogeology' | 'testProgram'> | null | undefined,
): IrHydrogeologyRow[] {
  const currentAquifer = raw.aquifer || null;

  if (existing == null) {
    if (!currentAquifer) return [];
    const action = resolveRowAction(irSeq);
    return [
      {
        action,
        ir_seq: irSeq,
        st_long_cd: currentAquifer,
        est_sta_water_lvl: raw.estimatedWaterLevelFt,
        est_water_quality: raw.estimatedTdsPpm,
        est_productivity: raw.estimatedRateGpm,
        flowing: null,
        h2s: raw.h2sPpm != null ? `${raw.h2sPpm} ppm` : null,
      },
    ];
  }

  const existingAquifer = existing.hydrogeology.aquifer || null;
  const rows: IrHydrogeologyRow[] = [];

  if (existingAquifer && existingAquifer !== currentAquifer) {
    rows.push({
      action: 'DELETE',
      ir_seq: irSeq,
      st_long_cd: existingAquifer,
      est_sta_water_lvl: null,
      est_water_quality: null,
      est_productivity: null,
      flowing: null,
      h2s: null,
    });
  }

  if (currentAquifer) {
    const isExisting = existingAquifer === currentAquifer;
    const action: IrRowAction = isExisting
      ? hydrogeologyRowUnchanged(existing.hydrogeology, existing.testProgram, raw)
        ? ''
        : 'UPDATE'
      : 'INSERT';
    rows.push({
      action,
      ir_seq: isExisting ? irSeq : null,
      st_long_cd: currentAquifer,
      est_sta_water_lvl: raw.estimatedWaterLevelFt,
      est_water_quality: raw.estimatedTdsPpm,
      est_productivity: raw.estimatedRateGpm,
      flowing: null,
      h2s: raw.h2sPpm != null ? `${raw.h2sPpm} ppm` : null,
    });
  }

  return rows;
}

const isBlankOffsetWell = (row: WwellIrRawOffsetWell): boolean => row.epANum == null && !row.aquifer;

/** Natural key exad_gwd_ir_water is matched on — (ir_seq, ep_a_num) is its primary key. */
const waterKey = (epANum: number): string => String(epANum);

/** Only the columns save_water's UPDATE branch writes — ep_a_num is the match key itself. */
const waterRowUnchanged = (existingRow: IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell, row: WwellIrRawOffsetWell): boolean =>
  (existingRow.distanceKm ?? null) === row.distanceKm &&
  (existingRow.location ?? null) === (row.location || null) &&
  (existingRow.aquifer ?? null) === (row.aquifer || null) &&
  (existingRow.totalDepthFt ?? null) === row.totalDepthFt &&
  (existingRow.testDurationMin ?? null) === row.testDurationMin &&
  (existingRow.rateGpm ?? null) === row.rateGpm &&
  (existingRow.rpm ?? null) === row.rpm &&
  (existingRow.swlFt ?? null) === row.swlFt &&
  (existingRow.pwlFt ?? null) === row.pwlFt &&
  (existingRow.drawdownFt ?? null) === row.drawdownFt &&
  (existingRow.specificCapacity ?? null) === row.specificCapacity &&
  (existingRow.tdsPpm ?? null) === row.tdsPpm &&
  (existingRow.h2sPpm ?? null) === row.h2sPpm;

/** `temp` is the one exad_gwd_ir_water column the form has no field for — always sent null. */
const toWaterRow = (row: WwellIrRawOffsetWell, action: IrRowAction, irSeq: number | null): IrWaterRow => ({
  action,
  ir_seq: irSeq,
  ep_a_num: row.epANum ?? 0,
  distanct: row.distanceKm,
  direction: row.location || null,
  aquifer: row.aquifer,
  td: row.totalDepthFt,
  rate: row.rateGpm,
  rpm: row.rpm,
  swl: row.swlFt,
  final_pwl: row.pwlFt,
  drawdown: row.drawdownFt,
  spec_capacity: row.specificCapacity,
  temp: null,
  field_tds: row.tdsPpm,
  test_duration: row.testDurationMin,
  h2s: row.h2sPpm,
});

function buildWaterRows(
  raw: readonly WwellIrRawOffsetWell[],
  irSeq: number | null,
  existing: readonly IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell[] | null | undefined,
): IrWaterRow[] {
  const currentRows = raw.filter(row => !isBlankOffsetWell(row));

  if (existing == null) {
    const action = resolveRowAction(irSeq);
    return currentRows.map(row => toWaterRow(row, action, irSeq));
  }

  const existingByKey = new Map(existing.map(row => [waterKey(row.epANum), row]));
  const keptKeys = new Set<string>();

  const upserts: IrWaterRow[] = currentRows.map(row => {
    const key = waterKey(row.epANum ?? 0);
    const existingRow = existingByKey.get(key);
    if (existingRow) keptKeys.add(key);
    const action: IrRowAction = existingRow ? (waterRowUnchanged(existingRow, row) ? '' : 'UPDATE') : 'INSERT';
    return toWaterRow(row, action, existingRow ? irSeq : null);
  });

  const deletes: IrWaterRow[] = existing
    .filter(row => !keptKeys.has(waterKey(row.epANum)))
    .map(row => toWaterRow(offsetWellToRaw(row), 'DELETE', irSeq));

  return [...upserts, ...deletes];
}

/** Domain → raw shape, so a DELETE row is built by the same toWaterRow() as every other action. */
const offsetWellToRaw = (row: IntegratedRequirementOffsetWell): WwellIrRawOffsetWell => ({
  epANum: row.epANum,
  distanceKm: row.distanceKm ?? null,
  location: row.location ?? '',
  aquifer: row.aquifer ?? '',
  totalDepthFt: row.totalDepthFt ?? null,
  testDurationMin: row.testDurationMin ?? null,
  rateGpm: row.rateGpm ?? null,
  rpm: row.rpm ?? null,
  swlFt: row.swlFt ?? null,
  pwlFt: row.pwlFt ?? null,
  drawdownFt: row.drawdownFt ?? null,
  specificCapacity: row.specificCapacity ?? null,
  tdsPpm: row.tdsPpm ?? null,
  h2sPpm: row.h2sPpm ?? null,
});

/** exad_gwd_ir_header.tops_remarks is a single column, but Formation Tops is a repeatable row
 *  list each with its own "Remarks" text — join whatever's there instead of keeping only one row's
 *  text and silently dropping the rest. */
function buildTopsRemarks(rows: readonly WwellIrRawFormationTop[]): string | null {
  const remarks = rows.map(row => row.remarks?.trim()).filter((text): text is string => !!text);
  return remarks.length ? remarks.join('; ') : null;
}

/** TEMPORARY: resolves a geologist's display name to the numeric id
 *  exad_gwd_ir_header.cont_geologist/sub_cont_geologist actually expects — see
 *  WWELL_GEOLOGIST_ID_BY_NAME's doc comment for why this is a stopgap. */
function resolveGeologistId(name: string): number | null {
  return name ? (WWELL_GEOLOGIST_ID_BY_NAME[name] ?? null) : null;
}

/** exad_gwd_ir_header.trns_type is VARCHAR2(10) — too short for the full Transmission label, so a
 *  known option maps to its short code; anything else (free-typed 'combo' text) is truncated to
 *  fit rather than silently dropped by Oracle's lax JSON_TABLE conversion. */
function resolveTransmissionCode(value: string): string | null {
  if (!value) return null;
  return WWELL_TRANSMISSION_CODE_BY_LABEL[value] ?? value.slice(0, 10);
}

/** Bridges the wizard's raw form value to the exad_gwd_ir_* bulk-save wire format — see wwell-ir-payload.model.ts. */
export function buildWwellIrBulkSavePayload(
  raw: WwellIrFormRawValue,
  context: WwellIrSaveContext = NEW_IR_CONTEXT,
): WwellIrBulkSavePayload {
  const { irSeq, existing } = context;
  const headerAction = resolveRowAction(irSeq);

  const header: IrHeaderRow = {
    action: headerAction,
    ir_seq: irSeq,
    prewap_seq: raw.reference.main.linkedRequestId ? Number(raw.reference.main.linkedRequestId) : null,
    ltr_no: raw.reference.main.letterNumber || null,
    screen: '',
    sampling: raw.programDetails.developmentTesting.sampling || null,
    logging: raw.programDetails.developmentTesting.logging || null,
    tops_remarks: buildTopsRemarks(raw.subsurface.formationTops),
    publish_dt: null,
    publish_flg: 'N', // flips once the approval workflow publishes this record, not at authoring time
    curr_flg: 'Y',
    publish_by: null,
    cont_geologist: resolveGeologistId(raw.contacts.geologist.geologistPrimary),
    sub_cont_geologist: resolveGeologistId(raw.contacts.geologist.geologistSecondary),
    water_remarks: null,
    gen_remarks: null,
    completion: raw.programDetails.main.completion || null,
    cmpl_type: null,
    acidization: raw.programDetails.main.acidization || null,
    trns_type: resolveTransmissionCode(raw.programDetails.main.transmission),
    dt_rmks: raw.programDetails.developmentTesting.remarks || null,
    mud_rmks: raw.programDetails.developmentTesting.mudLogging || null,
    test_type: raw.hydrogeology.main.testType || null,
    flow_rmks: raw.programDetails.developmentTesting.flowTest || null,
  };

  const casing = buildCasingRows(raw.subsurface.casing, irSeq, existing?.casing);
  const tops = buildTopsRows(raw.subsurface.formationTops, irSeq, existing?.formationTops);
  const hydrogeology = buildHydrogeologyRows(raw.hydrogeology.main, irSeq, existing);
  const water = buildWaterRows(raw.contacts.offsetWells, irSeq, existing?.offsetWells);

  return [
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_header', data: [header] },
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_casing', data: casing.length ? casing : null },
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_hydrogeology', data: hydrogeology.length ? hydrogeology : null },
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_tops', data: tops.length ? tops : null },
    { table: 'exad_gwd_ir_water', data: water.length ? water : null },
  ];
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-summary.mapper.ts

```ts
import { IntegratedRequirement, IntegratedRequirementTestType } from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { todayIsoDate } from '../../shared/wwell-approvals/wwell-approval-request.util';
import { mockApprovalChainForIrSeq } from './wwell-ir-chain.mock';
import { IrHeaderSummary } from './wwell-ir-payload.model';

/**
 * Backend list rows are backed only by exad_gwd_ir_header — the domain fields the UI displays
 * (client, wellName, contacts, casing...) have no column there yet, so they're left blank rather
 * than guessed. Extend once the header table carries those fields (see IrHeaderSummary).
 */
export function mapIrHeaderSummaryToIntegratedRequirement(row: IrHeaderSummary): IntegratedRequirement {
  const code = `IR-${row.irSeq}`;

  return {
    id: code,
    code,
    irSeq: row.irSeq,
    // Neither date column is guaranteed to be populated on exad_gwd_ir_header (see the real
    // payload that motivated this: every row came back with both createdDt and updatedDt null) —
    // fall back to today rather than crashing the whole list map on a null .slice() call.
    updatedDate: (row.updatedDt ?? row.createdDt ?? todayIsoDate()).slice(0, 10),
    // publish_flg is the only status-shaped signal this table carries — collapses the domain
    // model's finer-grained statuses (draft/in-progress/submit-for-review/in-review) into one.
    status: row.publishFlg === 'Y' ? 'approved' : 'in-progress',
    requestReference: {
      // prewap_seq/ltr_no are real exad_gwd_ir_header columns (unlike wellName/coordinates/etc.
      // below) — read straight off the list row so a record's "Link to Request" is known
      // immediately from the list. Water Well Name/Coordinates/Surface Elevation stay blank until
      // detail is fetched (see mergeIrDetailBlocksIntoIntegratedRequirement's exad_rcd_prewap
      // block) — the list endpoint has no join for them.
      linkedRequestId: row.prewapSeq != null ? String(row.prewapSeq) : null,
      letterNumber: row.ltrNo ?? null,
      client: '',
      supportingBusiness: '',
      wellName: '',
      targetAquifer: '',
      targetDepthFt: 0,
      surfaceElevationFtAsl: null,
      coordinates: '',
    },
    formationTops: [],
    casing: [],
    programDetails: {
      completion: row.cmplType ?? '',
      openHoleSizeIn: null,
      acidization: '',
      transmission: '',
      developmentTesting: { logging: '', mudLogging: '', sampling: '', flowTest: '', remarks: '' },
    },
    contacts: { geologistPrimary: '', geologistSecondary: null },
    offsetWells: [],
    offsetWellsRemarks: null,
    hydrogeology: { aquifer: '', estimatedTdsPpm: null, h2sPpm: null, estimatedPumpDepthFt: null },
    testProgram: {
      testType: (row.testType as IntegratedRequirementTestType | null) ?? 'flow-test',
      estimatedRateGpm: null,
      estimatedWaterLevelFt: null,
    },
    attachmentNames: [],
    // No approval-process API yet either — preview the same mocked timeline the detail-selection
    // fetch below will return, so the list row's mini-chain matches what opening it shows.
    chain: mockApprovalChainForIrSeq(row.irSeq),
  };
}

export function mapIrHeaderSummariesToIntegratedRequirements(
  rows: readonly IrHeaderSummary[],
): IntegratedRequirement[] {
  return rows.map(mapIrHeaderSummaryToIntegratedRequirement);
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-form/wwell-ir-wellbore-diagram.mapper.ts

```ts
import { WellboreDiagramData } from '@models/well-design/wellbore-diagram.model';
import {
  IntegratedRequirement,
  IntegratedRequirementCasingRow,
  IntegratedRequirementFormationTop,
} from '@models/wwell-planning/integrated-requirement.model';
import { ICasingIR } from '@shared/models/wwell/casing-ir.model';
import { IDrlgCsg } from '@shared/models/wwell/drlg-csg.model';
import { ITopsIR } from '@shared/models/wwell/tops-ir.model';
import { IWellDesign } from '@shared/models/wwell/well-design.model';
import { parseInchSize } from '@shared/utils/wellbore-math.util';
import {
  WwellIrFormRawValue,
  WwellIrRawCasingRow,
  WwellIrRawFormationTop,
  WwellIrRawHydrology,
  WwellIrRawProgramMain,
} from './wwell-ir-form.types';

/**
 * Adapts IR data into the `WellboreDiagramData` shape the shared `app-well-bore-view` component
 * (presentation/well-bore-view) expects — so both the wizard (live, still-being-edited raw form
 * value) and the read-only IR detail page (a submitted `IntegratedRequirement`) can embed that
 * component unmodified: the wizard as an always-animated "Well Design View" step
 * (`buildWellboreDiagramData`), the detail page as a static as-submitted diagram
 * (`buildWellboreDiagramDataFromIntegratedRequirement`).
 *
 * An Integrated Requirement is always a plan, never an as-drilled record, so no `drlgCasings`
 * entry produced here ever carries a real bottom depth (`wCsgBotDpth`/`wLnrBotDepth`).
 * `well-bore-view`'s renderers only switch a casing/liner to the green "actual" gradient when
 * a matching `drlgCasings` row has a real bottom depth — so every shape here always renders
 * with the steel/silver "planned" gradient, even though `drlgCasings` is no longer always
 * empty (see `buildLinerTopAnchors` below).
 */

const LINER_TYPE = 'Liner';

/**
 * A freshly added (or still-mid-edit) Casing/Formation Top row starts out with empty/null
 * field values — but `mat-select`/number inputs still exist and produce SOME value the moment
 * a user makes any single selection (e.g. just picking "Liner" from the Type dropdown before
 * touching Size or Depth). Mapping every row unconditionally meant a half-filled row already
 * rendered a shape/label on the diagram (a "Liner @ 0" annotation with no real size or depth
 * behind it) as soon as it existed, rather than once it actually described something real. These
 * gates require every field the diagram actually draws from before a row counts — reused by both
 * the Subsurface & Construction step's live preview and the Well Design View step (both call
 * `buildWellboreDiagramData`).
 */
function isRenderableCasingRow(row: WwellIrRawCasingRow): boolean {
  return !!row.type && !!row.sizeIn && row.depthFtBgl !== null && row.depthFtBgl !== undefined;
}

function isRenderableFormationTop(row: WwellIrRawFormationTop): boolean {
  return !!row.name && row.depthFt !== null && row.depthFt !== undefined;
}

const COMPLETION_OPEN_HOLE = 'Open Hole';
const COMPLETION_SCREENED = 'Screened';
const COMPLETION_SCREENED_GRAVEL_PACK = 'Screened + Gravel Pack';
const COMPLETION_LINER_PERFORATED = 'Liner + Perforated';

/** Anchors the depth scale to a stable minimum so a shallow/partial entry doesn't stretch
 * across the full drawing height, and so the scale's domain is never degenerate ([0, 0]) before the
 * (required, but non-linear-stepper-skippable) target depth field is filled in. */
const MIN_SCALE_DEPTH_FT = 500;

/**
 * Never used to match a real casing — `casing.renderer.ts` / `wellbore-depth.helpers.ts`
 * match `drlgCasings` rows to casings by `wCsgOdSz === csg.csgSize`, so this sentinel must be
 * a value no user-entered casing size could ever equal. Only `buildLinerTopAnchors`' rows use
 * this; they're read exclusively through `wLnrOdSz`, never `wCsgOdSz`.
 */
const LINER_ANCHOR_SENTINEL = '__wwell_ir_liner_anchor__';

/**
 * `casing.renderer.ts`'s `getCasingTier` derives each casing's on-screen half-width purely
 * from its position in this array via `indexOf` (tier 0 = narrowest, each later tier wider) —
 * it never reads `csgSize` for width, only for the text label. It also paints casings in this
 * same array order, later entries on top, so whichever entry ends up widest is also drawn last
 * — the standard "wide, shallow casing painted over a narrow, deep one" telescoping look.
 *
 * Sorting by SIZE (ascending — smallest first, so the largest entered size lands last/widest)
 * is what makes width actually track the number the user typed, rather than depth order. For
 * well-formed data (size decreasing with depth, real telescoping construction) this produces
 * the exact same order as depth-descending would — size and depth are inversely correlated
 * there, so there's no behavior change for the common case. It only diverges for physically
 * inverted rows (a deeper casing entered wider than a shallower one, flagged separately by
 * `findInvertedCasingSizeRows` in wwell-ir-casing-warnings.util.ts) — there, the wider-but-
 * deeper entry now paints last and can fully cover the narrower-but-shallower one in their
 * overlapping depth range, since two casings can't actually nest that way in reality either.
 * That's an unavoidable consequence of a genuinely invalid combination, not a rendering bug —
 * see `casing.renderer.ts` (off-limits to edit) for why: `buildCasingPath` always draws a
 * casing as a full tube from ground level (0) to its own depth, so the only way to reflect its
 * real entered size is via this array's order.
 *
 * Unparseable sizes sort as 0in (narrowest/first) — the safest fallback, since
 * `isRenderableCasingRow` already guarantees `sizeIn` is non-empty text, just not necessarily
 * a `parseInchSize`-recognizable number.
 */
function mapCasings(rows: readonly WwellIrRawCasingRow[]): ICasingIR[] {
  const casings: ICasingIR[] = rows.map(row => ({
    csgSize: row.sizeIn,
    csgType: row.type,
    csgDepth: row.depthFtBgl ?? 0,
    csgRemarks: row.remarks || null,
  }));
  return [...casings].sort((a, b) => (parseInchSize(a.csgSize) ?? 0) - (parseInchSize(b.csgSize) ?? 0));
}

/**
 * The wizard's "Top Of Liner (TOL)" field (`tolFtBgl`, shown only for Liner rows) is optional —
 * most IR liners never get one filled in. `liner.renderer.ts`'s `rawLinersFromIR` only treats
 * `wLnrTopDepth` as a genuine top-of-liner value (and shows the "TOL @ ..." label/tooltip row)
 * when `LinerEntry.hasActualTop` is true, which it derives straight from whether this anchor's
 * `wLnrTopDepth` is a real positive number — so this must leave `wLnrTopDepth` unset (`null`)
 * whenever the user hasn't entered one, rather than silently filling it with a computed guess
 * that would then get mislabeled as a real measured depth.
 *
 * This builds synthetic `IDrlgCsg` "anchor" entries carrying ONLY `wLnrTopDepth` (when
 * provided) per liner — never `wLnrBotDepth` or a real `wCsgOdSz` — because `rawLinersFromIR`
 * gates `hasActual` (and therefore the green-vs-silver color choice) strictly on
 * `wLnrBotDepth`, and gates the casing-actual lookup strictly on `wCsgOdSz`. Leaving both unset
 * keeps every casing and liner silver.
 *
 * When TOL is left blank, `rawLinersFromIR` falls back to its own `chainTop` chaining instead:
 * the shallowest liner hangs from the deepest structural casing's shoe; each deeper liner hangs
 * from the liner immediately above it (matching the physical assumption already baked into
 * `renderLinerShapes`'s `hangerY`). Casing rows whose type is Conductor, Surface, Intermediate,
 * or Production are all "structural" here — anything that isn't `'Liner'`, exactly matching how
 * casing.renderer.ts itself distinguishes casing from liner (`csgType !== 'Liner'`).
 *
 * An explicit `tolFtBgl` doesn't otherwise perturb that chain: the next liner's fallback top is
 * still this row's own `depthFtBgl` (its shoe), not its `tolFtBgl`, since a liner physically
 * hangs from the casing/liner above it regardless of how far down its own top was entered.
 */
function buildLinerTopAnchors(rows: readonly WwellIrRawCasingRow[]): IDrlgCsg[] {
  const linersShallowToDeep = rows
    .filter(row => row.type === LINER_TYPE)
    .slice()
    .sort((a, b) => (a.depthFtBgl ?? 0) - (b.depthFtBgl ?? 0));

  return linersShallowToDeep.map(row => ({
    wCsgOdSz: LINER_ANCHOR_SENTINEL,
    wCsgBotDpth: 0,
    wLnrOdSz: row.sizeIn,
    wLnrTopDepth: row.tolFtBgl ?? null,
    wLnrBotDepth: null,
  }));
}

function mapGeologicTops(
  rows: readonly WwellIrRawFormationTop[],
  surfaceElevationFtAsl: number | null,
): ITopsIR[] {
  return rows.map(row => {
    const planTvdDepth = row.depthFt ?? 0;
    return {
      stLongCd: row.name,
      planTvdDepth,
      planSsDepth: surfaceElevationFtAsl !== null ? surfaceElevationFtAsl - planTvdDepth : 0,
    };
  });
}

const FLOW_TEST_TYPE = 'flow-test';

/**
 * Derives the water-level fields `water-level.renderer.ts`'s `renderWaterLevel` reads —
 * `wellDesign.staWaterLvl` (a bare number, the actual depth it draws the line at) and
 * `wellDesign.swLvlTxt` (free text: presence of "flow" anywhere in it is that renderer's own
 * flag for "this is a flowing well, not a static level" — see its `isFlowing` check — and for a
 * flowing well it also re-parses the psi value straight back out of this same string via
 * `parseSiwhpPsi`, rather than taking a separate numeric field).
 *
 * Mirrors the Estimated Hydrology step's own test-type split (see `buildHydrogeologyRows` in
 * wwell-ir-detail-rows.util.ts): a flow test's wellhead pressure (SIWHP) draws as an above-ground
 * riser, a pump test's estimated water level draws as a below-ground line — the renderer can only
 * ever draw one of the two per well, same as the form only ever collects one.
 */
function buildWaterLevelFields(hydrology: WwellIrRawHydrology): Pick<IWellDesign, 'staWaterLvl' | 'swLvlTxt'> {
  if (hydrology.testType === FLOW_TEST_TYPE) {
    return hydrology.siwhpPsi !== null
      ? { staWaterLvl: null, swLvlTxt: `Flowing — SIWHP: ${hydrology.siwhpPsi} psi` }
      : { staWaterLvl: null, swLvlTxt: null };
  }

  return hydrology.estimatedWaterLevelFt !== null
    ? { staWaterLvl: hydrology.estimatedWaterLevelFt, swLvlTxt: `Static WL: ${hydrology.estimatedWaterLevelFt} ft` }
    : { staWaterLvl: null, swLvlTxt: null };
}

/**
 * Derives completion-element flags/remarks from the "Completion" dropdown (Program
 * Details step) plus, for Open Hole, the conditionally-shown "Open Hole Size" field.
 * `well-bore-view`'s renderers gate Open Hole strictly on a parseable inch size in
 * `ohRemarks`, and Gravel Pack / Liner Screen strictly on non-empty remarks text — the
 * constant fallback labels below exist only to satisfy those gates when the dropdown
 * implies the element but no free-text remarks field exists for it in this form.
 *
 * "Liner + Perforated" only actually renders once a Casing-table Liner row is exactly 7"
 * OD — that 7" convention is hardcoded inside liner.renderer.ts (PRE_PERF_LINER_SIZE_IN)
 * and is intentionally left untouched here.
 *
 * Water level / pump depth (`pump-and-depth.renderer.ts`'s `renderPump`, gated on `pumpLvl`)
 * come from the Estimated Hydrology step instead — see `buildWaterLevelFields` above.
 * `estimatedPumpDepthFt` is only ever populated for a pump test (see the field's own
 * `visibleWhen` in wwell-ir-form.schema.ts), so it's naturally `null` — no pump drawn — on a
 * flow-test record.
 */
function buildWellDesignFromForm(programMain: WwellIrRawProgramMain, hydrology: WwellIrRawHydrology): IWellDesign {
  const completion = programMain.completion;
  const isOpenHole = completion === COMPLETION_OPEN_HOLE;
  const isGravelPack = completion === COMPLETION_SCREENED_GRAVEL_PACK;
  const isScreened = completion === COMPLETION_SCREENED || isGravelPack;
  const isLinerPerforated = completion === COMPLETION_LINER_PERFORATED;

  return {
    name: null,
    prewapSeq: null,
    ...buildWaterLevelFields(hydrology),
    pumpLvl: hydrology.estimatedPumpDepthFt,
    ohFlg: isOpenHole ? 'Y' : 'N',
    ohRemarks: isOpenHole ? (programMain.openHoleSizeIn || null) : null,
    gpFlg: isGravelPack ? 'Y' : 'N',
    gpRemarks: isGravelPack ? 'Gravel Pack' : null,
    perforatedFlg: isLinerPerforated ? 'Y' : 'N',
    perforatedRemarks: isLinerPerforated ? '7" Pre-Perforated Liner' : null,
    lsFlg: isScreened ? 'Y' : 'N',
    lsRemarks: isScreened ? 'Screen' : null,
  };
}

function computeDisplayDepthFt(
  targetDepthFt: number,
  casingRows: readonly WwellIrRawCasingRow[],
  formationRows: readonly WwellIrRawFormationTop[],
): number {
  const depths = [
    targetDepthFt,
    ...casingRows.map(row => row.depthFtBgl ?? 0),
    ...formationRows.map(row => row.depthFt ?? 0),
  ];
  return Math.max(...depths, MIN_SCALE_DEPTH_FT);
}

/** Shared tail for both entry points below — takes already-normalized raw rows so the actual
 *  gating/mapping (isRenderable*, mapCasings, buildLinerTopAnchors, mapGeologicTops,
 *  buildWellDesignFromForm) is written once regardless of whether the source was a live,
 *  still-being-edited form value or a submitted `IntegratedRequirement`. */
function assembleWellboreDiagramData(
  wellName: string,
  targetDepthFt: number,
  casingRows: readonly WwellIrRawCasingRow[],
  formationRows: readonly WwellIrRawFormationTop[],
  surfaceElevationFtAsl: number | null,
  programMain: WwellIrRawProgramMain,
  hydrology: WwellIrRawHydrology,
): WellboreDiagramData {
  const renderableCasings = casingRows.filter(isRenderableCasingRow);
  const renderableFormationTops = formationRows.filter(isRenderableFormationTop);

  return {
    wellName,
    totalDepth: targetDepthFt,
    displayDepth: computeDisplayDepthFt(targetDepthFt, casingRows, formationRows),
    casings: mapCasings(renderableCasings),
    drlgCasings: buildLinerTopAnchors(renderableCasings),
    geologicTops: mapGeologicTops(renderableFormationTops, surfaceElevationFtAsl),
    hydrogeology: null,
    prewap: null,
    rigActivity: null,
    currentDepth: 0,
    targetDepth: targetDepthFt,
    mudCirculation: [],
    wellDesign: buildWellDesignFromForm(programMain, hydrology),
  };
}

/**
 * Gates the *entire* diagram — depth scale, ground level line, and total-depth label included,
 * not just individual casing/formation-top shapes (see `isRenderableCasingRow`/
 * `isRenderableFormationTop` above). Before any row is complete, `well-bore-view` still draws
 * that scaffold around nothing: a bare 0–500ft ruler, a "GROUND LEVEL" line, and a
 * "TOTAL DEPTH: 0 FT" label with no actual construction data behind any of it. Callers should
 * check this first and render an empty state instead of the diagram until it's true.
 */
export function hasWellboreDiagramData(raw: WwellIrFormRawValue): boolean {
  return (
    raw.subsurface.casing.some(isRenderableCasingRow) ||
    raw.subsurface.formationTops.some(isRenderableFormationTop)
  );
}

export function buildWellboreDiagramData(raw: WwellIrFormRawValue): WellboreDiagramData {
  return assembleWellboreDiagramData(
    raw.reference.main.wellName,
    raw.reference.main.targetDepthFt ?? 0,
    raw.subsurface.casing,
    raw.subsurface.formationTops,
    raw.reference.main.surfaceElevationFtAsl,
    raw.programDetails.main,
    raw.hydrogeology.main,
  );
}

/** `IntegratedRequirementCasingRow`/`FormationTop`/`ProgramDetails` are already-resolved
 *  (non-nullable) counterparts of the wizard's raw row types — these just fill in the raw
 *  shape's optional-text fields (`remarks`/`openHoleSizeIn`) with `''` in place
 *  of `null`, since a submitted IR's `null` here means the same "nothing entered" as the
 *  wizard's `''`. */
function toRawCasingRows(rows: readonly IntegratedRequirementCasingRow[]): WwellIrRawCasingRow[] {
  return rows.map(row => ({
    type: row.type,
    sizeIn: row.sizeIn,
    depthFtBgl: row.depthFtBgl,
    tolFtBgl: row.tolFtBgl,
    remarks: row.remarks ?? '',
  }));
}

function toRawFormationRows(rows: readonly IntegratedRequirementFormationTop[]): WwellIrRawFormationTop[] {
  return rows.map(row => ({
    name: row.name,
    depthFt: row.depthFt,
    subSeaFtAsl: row.subSeaFtAsl ?? null,
    remarks: row.remarks ?? '',
  }));
}

function toRawProgramMain(ir: IntegratedRequirement): WwellIrRawProgramMain {
  return {
    completion: ir.programDetails.completion,
    openHoleSizeIn: ir.programDetails.openHoleSizeIn ?? '',
    acidization: ir.programDetails.acidization,
    transmission: ir.programDetails.transmission,
  };
}

/** `IntegratedRequirementHydrogeology`/`TestProgram` are two separate domain groups that the
 *  wizard's single "Estimated Hydrology" step (and its one flat `WwellIrRawHydrology` raw shape —
 *  see wwell-ir-form.types.ts) collects together — recombine them here the same way. */
function toRawHydrology(ir: IntegratedRequirement): WwellIrRawHydrology {
  return {
    testType: ir.testProgram.testType,
    aquifer: ir.hydrogeology.aquifer,
    siwhpPsi: ir.testProgram.siwhpPsi ?? null,
    estimatedTdsPpm: ir.hydrogeology.estimatedTdsPpm,
    estimatedRateGpm: ir.testProgram.estimatedRateGpm,
    h2sPpm: ir.hydrogeology.h2sPpm,
    estimatedWaterLevelFt: ir.testProgram.estimatedWaterLevelFt,
    estimatedPumpDepthFt: ir.hydrogeology.estimatedPumpDepthFt,
  };
}

/** Same emptiness gate as `hasWellboreDiagramData`, for the IR editor/detail page. `liveCasingRows`/
 *  `liveFormationRows` let the editor substitute its Casing/Formation Tops sections' in-progress
 *  FormArray values while those sections are open for editing — pass `null`/omit for either one
 *  still reads straight from `ir`. */
export function hasWellboreDiagramDataForIntegratedRequirement(
  ir: IntegratedRequirement,
  liveCasingRows?: readonly WwellIrRawCasingRow[] | null,
  liveFormationRows?: readonly WwellIrRawFormationTop[] | null,
): boolean {
  const casingRows = liveCasingRows ?? toRawCasingRows(ir.casing);
  const formationRows = liveFormationRows ?? toRawFormationRows(ir.formationTops);
  return casingRows.some(isRenderableCasingRow) || formationRows.some(isRenderableFormationTop);
}

/** Detail/editor-page counterpart of `buildWellboreDiagramData` — same live `app-well-bore-view`
 *  diagram, fed from a submitted `IntegratedRequirement` instead of the in-progress wizard form,
 *  so those pages show the real engineering rendering instead of the lighter
 *  `wwell-ir-design-preview` sketch. See `hasWellboreDiagramDataForIntegratedRequirement` above
 *  for what `liveCasingRows`/`liveFormationRows` are for — the editor passes its Casing/Formation
 *  Tops sections' live FormArray values while they're open, so the preview updates as rows are
 *  added/edited instead of only once that section is saved.
 *
 *  `liveProgramMain`/`liveHydrology` are the same idea for Program Details (Completion — the
 *  element flags in `buildWellDesignFromForm`) and Estimated Hydrology (static water level / SIWHP
 *  / pump depth): without these, picking a different Completion or test type while that section is
 *  open wouldn't move the diagram until the whole record was saved, unlike casing/formation-top
 *  edits, which already updated it live. */
export function buildWellboreDiagramDataFromIntegratedRequirement(
  ir: IntegratedRequirement,
  liveCasingRows?: readonly WwellIrRawCasingRow[] | null,
  liveFormationRows?: readonly WwellIrRawFormationTop[] | null,
  liveProgramMain?: WwellIrRawProgramMain | null,
  liveHydrology?: WwellIrRawHydrology | null,
): WellboreDiagramData {
  return assembleWellboreDiagramData(
    ir.requestReference.wellName,
    ir.requestReference.targetDepthFt,
    liveCasingRows ?? toRawCasingRows(ir.casing),
    liveFormationRows ?? toRawFormationRows(ir.formationTops),
    ir.requestReference.surfaceElevationFtAsl,
    liveProgramMain ?? toRawProgramMain(ir),
    liveHydrology ?? toRawHydrology(ir),
  );
}
```

## [M] src/app/features/wwell-planning/wwell-ir/wwell-ir-view/wwell-ir-view-kv-card/wwell-ir-view-kv-card.component.scss

```scss
@use '../../../shared/wwell-design-tokens' as tokens;

$navy-deep: tokens.$navy-deep;
$muted: tokens.$muted;
$subtle: tokens.$subtle;
$border-soft: tokens.$border-soft;
$surface: tokens.$surface;
$surface-alt: tokens.$surface-alt;
$accent: tokens.$accent;

:host {
  display: block;
}

.kv-card {
  background: $surface;
  border: 1px solid #e8edf4;
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 1rem 1.1rem;
  box-shadow: 0 1px 3px rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.04);
}

.kv-card__title {
  margin: 0 0 0.75rem;
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
  font-weight: 700;
  color: $accent;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  letter-spacing: 0.05em;
}

// Column-major two-column layout — see the component's `rowCount` doc comment for why this isn't
// plain row-major auto-placement.
.kv-card__grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr;
  grid-auto-flow: column;
  grid-template-rows: repeat(var(--kv-rows, 1), auto);
  column-gap: 2rem;
  row-gap: 0.6rem;
  margin: 0;
}

@media (max-width: 560px) {
  .kv-card__grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    grid-auto-flow: row;
    grid-template-rows: none;
  }
}

.kv-card__field {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 0.15rem;
  min-width: 0;

  dt {
    font-size: tokens.$text-2xs;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    letter-spacing: 0.04em;
    color: $subtle;
  }

  dd {
    margin: 0;
    font-size: tokens.$text-sm;
    color: $navy-deep;
    overflow-wrap: break-word;
    // A remarks field's saved value can carry real line breaks (e.g. Development/Testing,
    // Sampling) — without this, `\n` renders as a plain space and a 3-line remark collapses
    // back into one run-on line.
    white-space: pre-line;
  }

  &[data-tone='warning'] dd {
    color: #b45309;
    font-weight: 600;
  }

  &[data-tone='danger'] dd {
    color: #b91c1c;
    font-weight: 600;
  }

  &[data-tone='success'] dd {
    color: #15803d;
    font-weight: 600;
  }
}

.kv-card__value--badge {
  display: inline-flex;
  width: fit-content;
  padding: 0.15rem 0.55rem;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: $surface-alt;
  color: $navy-deep;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: tokens.$text-xs;
}

.kv-card__empty {
  grid-column: 1 / -1;
  margin: 0;
  color: $muted;
  font-size: tokens.$text-sm;
}
```
